> 68	| `{{PROJECT_SLUG}}_embed_failure` | embed-mcorch-node timeout | warn | `{node_id, retry_count}` |
> 69	| `{{PROJECT_SLUG}}_sla_breach` | Process SLA exceeded | warn | `{process_id, elapsed_ms, threshold_ms}` |
> 70	
> 71	### 5.2 Auto-Recovery Script Stubs
> 72	
> 73	```bash
> 74	#!/bin/bash
> 75	# scripts/self-heal-{{PROJECT_SLUG}}.sh
> 76	# Triggered by mega-brain-daemon when {{PROJECT_SLUG}}_fn_failure detected
> 77	
> 78	FUNCTION_NAME="{{EDGE_FUNCTION_NAME}}"
> 79	LOG_TABLE="infra_health_logs"
> 80	
> 81	echo "[$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)] Self-heal triggered for $FUNCTION_NAME"
> 82	
> 83	# Step 1: Check function status
> 84	STATUS=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" \
> 85	  "$SUPABASE_URL/functions/v1/$FUNCTION_NAME/health" \
> 86	  -H "Authorization: Bearer $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")
> 87	
> 88	if [ "$STATUS" != "200" ]; then
> 89	  echo "[WARN] Function unhealthy (status: $STATUS). Re-deploying..."
> 90	  npx supabase functions deploy "$FUNCTION_NAME"
> 91	fi
> 92	
> 93	# Step 2: Log recovery attempt
> 94	# (insert into infra_health_logs via Supabase REST API)
> 95	echo "[OK] Self-heal complete for $FUNCTION_NAME"
> 96	```

> 136	      replaced = true;

> 144	// ─── Viral Autopilot Fatia 1 — product-aware monetization (FR-VA-004) ──────────────────────────────────────
> 145	// The legacy monetizeContent() above scans for the 3 hardcoded regex SKUs. The viral pipeline instead features
> 146	// a SPECIFIC product the tenant chose (resolved from vm_affiliate_products), so monetization is DETERMINISTIC:
> 147	// it links the chosen product whether or not the LLM echoed its exact name, and works on text surfaces
> 148	// (LinkedIn/X) as well as HTML (article).
> 149	
> 150	/** A product the tenant chose to feature, resolved server-side from vm_affiliate_products (authoritative). */
> 151	export interface ChosenProduct {
> 152	  external_id: string;            // = vm_affiliate_products.external_id (the MLB id / JOIN key)
> 153	  name: string;
> 154	  price?: number | null;
> 155	  category?: string | null;
> 156	  image_url?: string | null;
> 157	  url?: string | null;            // canonical product_url (informational; the link is rebuilt per-owner)
> 158	}
> 159	
> 160	/** Escape a literal string for safe inclusion in a RegExp. */
> 161	function escapeRegExp(s: string): string {
> 162	  return s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
> 163	}
> 164	
> 165	/** Escape a string for safe inclusion in HTML text/attribute context (defense-in-depth: the product name is
> 166	 *  catalog data — service-role-written, not tenant-controllable — but never interpolated raw into HTML). */
> 167	function escapeHtml(s: string): string {
> 168	  return s.replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
> 169	}
> 170	
> 171	/** Deterministic CONVERSION-LEAK guard: LLMs frequently emit the final CTA linking to the bare brand homepage
> 172	 *  (https://mcorch.com) instead of the affiliate link — the single highest buy-intent click, leaked to a page that
> 173	 *  earns nothing (and is CF-challenged). Rewrite every homepage-only reference to the affiliate target so no buy
> 174	 *  intent escapes the funnel — in BOTH the HTML href form (articles) AND the BARE-TEXT URL form (LinkedIn/X posts,
> 175	 *  where the LLM writes "Garanta o seu aqui: https://mcorch.com" as plain text — witnessed leaking 2026-06-26 on a
> 176	 *  real autopilot post). Only the BARE homepage (optional trailing slash, no path) is rewritten — real deep links
> 177	 *  (mcorch.com/go/<id>, mcorch.com/article, ...) are left intact via the negative lookahead. */
> 178	export function rewriteHomepageCtas(content: string, affiliateTarget: string): string {
> 179	  if (!affiliateTarget) return content;
> 180	  return content
> 181	    // HTML href form (articles): href="https://mcorch.com" → href="<affiliate>"
> 182	    .replace(/href=(["'])https?:\/\/(?:www\.)?mcorch\.com\/?\1/gi, `href=$1${affiliateTarget}$1`)
> 183	    // Bare-text form (LinkedIn/X): a homepage URL not followed by a path/word char → <affiliate>. The lookahead
> 184	    // (?![\w/]) spares deep links (…/go, …/article) and the affiliateTarget itself (never the bare brand homepage).
> 185	    .replace(/https?:\/\/(?:www\.)?mcorch\.com\/?(?![\w/])/gi, affiliateTarget);
> 186	}
> 187	
> 188	/** Embed a monetized link for `productName` into `content`. HTML surfaces inline-link the first mention (or
> 189	 *  append an anchored CTA); text surfaces (LinkedIn/X) append a raw-URL CTA (an <a> tag would render literally,
> 190	 *  and inlining a URL mid-tweet risks the 280-char limit). Returns the rewritten content. */
> 191	function embedProductLink(content: string, productName: string, target: string, format: "html" | "text"): string {
> 192	  const name = (productName ?? "").trim();
> 193	  if (!name) return content;
> 194	  const re = new RegExp(escapeRegExp(name), "i");
> 195	  if (format === "html") {
> 196	    if (re.test(content)) {
> 197	      let replaced = false;
> 198	      return content.replace(re, (m) => {
> 199	        if (replaced) return m;
> 200	        replaced = true;
> 201	        return `<a href="${target}">${m}</a>`;
> 202	      });
> 203	    }
> 204	    return `${content}\n<p>👉 Confira o <a href="${target}">${escapeHtml(name)}</a> no Mercado Livre.</p>`;
> 205	  }
> 206	  // text (LinkedIn / X): always append a CTA line with the raw tracked URL.
> 207	  return `${content}\n\n👉 ${name}: ${target}`;
> 208	}
> 209	
> 210	/**
> 211	 * Monetize one creative for ONE chosen product. Builds the per-owner tracked ML link (buildMlProductUrl →
> 212	 * affiliate_links row whose content_id = the creative's content_library.id, fixing the historical null), then
> 213	 * embeds it. FAIL-OPEN: no affiliate_tag / no product → content unchanged. `guaranteeCta`: when true (the
> 214	 * featured/primary product) a CTA is appended if the name isn't textually present, so the primary product is
> 215	 * ALWAYS monetized; when false (secondary products) the link is added only if the product is actually mentioned,
> 216	 * to avoid orphan affiliate_links rows + link spam.
> 217	 */
> 218	export async function monetizeForProduct(
> 219	  admin: { from: (t: string) => any },
> 220	  supabaseUrl: string,
> 221	  content: string,
> 222	  ownerId: string,
> 223	  affiliateTag: string | null,
> 224	  product: ChosenProduct,
> 225	  opts: {
> 226	    campaignId?: string | null;
> 227	    mattTool?: string | null;
> 228	    contentVariantId?: string | null;
> 229	    platform?: string | null;
> 230	    format?: "html" | "text";
> 231	    guaranteeCta?: boolean;
> 232	  } = {},
> 233	): Promise<{ content: string; linked: number; linkId: string | null }> {
> 234	  if (!content || !affiliateTag || !product?.external_id) return { content, linked: 0, linkId: null };
> 235	
> 236	  const format = opts.format ?? "html";
> 237	  const guaranteeCta = opts.guaranteeCta ?? true;
> 238	  const name = (product.name ?? "").trim();
> 239	  const mentioned = name ? new RegExp(escapeRegExp(name), "i").test(content) : false;
> 240	
> 241	  // Skip silently when a secondary product isn't mentioned — no orphan row, no CTA clutter.
> 242	  if (!mentioned && !guaranteeCta) return { content, linked: 0, linkId: null };
> 243	
> 244	  const mlUrl = await buildMlProductUrl(admin, product.external_id, affiliateTag, opts.mattTool, ownerId);
> 245	  const signed = isMlShortLink(mlUrl);   // mlUrl IS the owner's definitive meli.la when present (sale-attributable)

> 17	
> 18	`content-provenance` é um **pós-processador de proveniência determinístico** — um host-worker (`provenance-bridge`, molde `video-bridge`) que marca **machine-readable** todo asset sintético (imagem/vídeo/voz) no ponto onde nasce (spine `creative_assets`), em **3 camadas de defesa-em-profundidade** (C1 C2PA · C2 watermark invisível · C3 metadata IPTC/XMP), **cada uma provada por `verify` antes de reportar sucesso**, **sem reconstruir distribuição**.
> 19	
> 20	Por isso os `yes` abaixo são exatamente os padrões que **a própria função do módulo encarna** — não há inflação. Padrões de coordenação multi-agente e de aprendizado/exploração são `n-a` ou `deferred` porque o marcador é single-worker determinístico por regra regulatória, não um coletivo nem um loop de reward.

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/docs/bok/product-opportunity-engine/01-mrd.md",
  "content": "# MRD \u2014 Market Requirements Document\n\n**Project:** `product-opportunity-engine`\n**Version:** v0.1 | **Date:** 2026-07-23\n**BoK Applied:** BABOK v3 (Strategy Analysis + Elicitation & Collaboration)\n**Status:** Sealed (derived from `00-deepsearch-blueprint.md`)\n\n---\n\n## 1. Executive Summary\n\nAfiliados e criadores de conte\u00fado enfrentam **dois custos escondidos** antes de qualquer real de comiss\u00e3o: (a) o **risco de investir tempo/dinheiro num produto morto** (sem demanda estrutural real), e (b) a **fric\u00e7\u00e3o de gerar o pr\u00f3prio link de afiliado compliant** em cada rede \u2014 cada rede com formato de tag, janela de cookie e pol\u00edtica de cloaking diferentes. O **Product Opportunity Engine (POE)** funde as duas metades num flywheel: um **funil de valida\u00e7\u00e3o org\u00e2nica gratuita** (nicho \u2192 produto \u2192 persona \u2192 avatar \u2192 conte\u00fado \u2192 workspace, gates ordenados progressivamente mais caros) que mata oportunidades ruins **antes** do gasto, acoplado a um **rail multi-afiliado per-user** que devolve ao usu\u00e1rio **o link de afiliado DELE** a partir de um link de produto original \u2014 come\u00e7ando pela Amazon Associates (Sovereign j\u00e1 aprovado), via a mec\u00e2nica mais simples e mais compliant que a pesquisa estabeleceu: **string-append `?tag=` sale-attributable**. O momento \u00e9 agora porque a premissa t\u00e9cnica original mudou: a **PA-API 5.0 morreu** (\u2192 Creators API), e o achado material \u00e9 que **a gera\u00e7\u00e3o de link nem precisa de API** \u2014 \u00e9 anexa\u00e7\u00e3o de par\u00e2metro server-side, custo USD = 0.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Market Problem Statement\n\n### 2.1 Current State\n\nO afiliado/criador hoje:\n- **Valida no escuro ou paga caro.** Ferramentas de valida\u00e7\u00e3o de produto (SellTheTrend, Ecomhunt, Glimpse) s\u00e3o **pagas**; o Sovereign proibiu explicitamente Apify pago. O stack gratuito real (Google Trends, Meta Ad Library, Movers & Shakers) existe mas \u00e9 **fragmentado, fr\u00e1gil e cheio de armadilhas de ToS/IP** \u2014 ningu\u00e9m o orquestra com honestidade de converg\u00eancia.\n- **Gera link manualmente, rede a rede.** Para o ML, o MCORCH j\u00e1 resolve o link per-user (`_shared/affiliate.ts` vivo). Para a Amazon, o usu\u00e1rio precisa logar no SiteStripe e copiar/colar \u2014 e a maioria **n\u00e3o sabe** que rotear o clique por um redirect pr\u00f3prio (o padr\u00e3o que o rail ML usa) **viola o ToS da Amazon**.\n- **Investe antes de saber se o p\u00fablico existe** \u2014 produz avatar, conte\u00fado e campanha para um produto que nunca teve tra\u00e7\u00e3o estrutural.\n\n### 2.2 Root Cause Analysis\n\n- **Gap de mercado:** as duas metades (validar demanda / gerar link) s\u00e3o vendidas por produtos diferentes, sem um flywheel que ligue *validar \u2192 gerar link \u2192 gerar criativo* num s\u00f3 espa\u00e7o reutiliz\u00e1vel, per-user, custo-zero-first.\n- **Gap de tecnologia:** o custo real de dados de produto (PA-API) sumiu (deprecada); o mercado ainda escreve integra\u00e7\u00f5es sobre a API morta. Poucos perceberam que o link b\u00e1sico Amazon **dispensa API**.\n- **Gap de processo/compliance:** as pol\u00edticas da Amazon (anti-scraping, anti-cloaking, own-tag-only) s\u00e3o invis\u00edveis ao usu\u00e1rio m\u00e9dio \u2014 copi\u00e1-las erroneamente do rail ML gera **ban de conta afiliada**.\n\n### 2.3 Desired State\n\nUm usu\u00e1rio do MCORCH: (1) informa um **nicho** OU cola um **link de produto original**; (2) recebe um **veredito material de demanda** ancorado em \u22652 sinais gratuitos convergentes com URLs citadas \u2014 ou um \"mate esta oportunidade, \u00e9 ru\u00eddo sazonal\"; (3) para oportunidades aprovadas, recebe **o link de afiliado dele** (ML: `meli.la`/`matt_word`; Amazon: `?tag=` cru, 100% compliant), persona, avatar e criativo gerados; (4) tudo empacotado num **workspace Spaces reutiliz\u00e1vel** + n\u00f3 na Knowledge Mesh. Nenhuma credencial persistida; nenhuma viola\u00e7\u00e3o de ToS; custo USD externo = 0 nos sinais.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Target Market Segments\n\n| Segment | Description | Size Estimate | Urgency | Accessibility |\n|---------|-------------|---------------|---------|---------------|\n| Primary | **Afiliado/criador solo** (Usu\u00e1rio Zero \u2192 Usu\u00e1rio 1..N) que j\u00e1 monetiza ML e quer somar Amazon + validar antes de investir | Nicho inicial: o pr\u00f3prio Sovereign (Usu\u00e1rio Zero) + coorte early-adopter da base MCORCH | High | Alta (j\u00e1 dentro da plataforma; rail ML provado) |\n| Secondary | **Ag\u00eancia/operador multi-produto** que roda N campanhas e precisa de atribui\u00e7\u00e3o de receita correta por produto/rede | Segmento de expans\u00e3o p\u00f3s-valida\u00e7\u00e3o Usu\u00e1rio Zero | Medium | M\u00e9dia (exige multi-rede maduro + Creators API) |\n\n### 3.1 TAM / SAM / SOM\n\n> Estimativas indicativas (Lei 1: n\u00e3o h\u00e1 survey prim\u00e1rio; derivadas de tamanho de programas de afiliado + base MCORCH). Marcadas como estimativa.\n\n| Metric | Value | Basis |\n|--------|-------|-------|\n| TAM | Global affiliate marketing ~US$ 15\u201320 bi/ano (estimativa de ind\u00fastria) | Programas Amazon Associates + ML + redes | \n| SAM | Criadores PT-BR que operam ML + Amazon e precisam de valida\u00e7\u00e3o gratuita | Subconjunto lus\u00f3fono acess\u00edvel pela UI PT-BR do MCORCH |\n| SOM (12m) | Usu\u00e1rio Zero + coorte early-adopter da base MCORCH (dezenas a centenas de tenants) | Capacidade de onboarding per-user fail-closed + gate Creators API |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Competitive Landscape\n\n| Competitor | Positioning | Strengths | Weaknesses | Our Differentiation |\n|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|---------------------|\n| **Amazon SiteStripe / `amzn.to`** (nativo) | Gera\u00e7\u00e3o de link oficial logado | Compliant por design; atribui venda | Manual; um produto por vez; sem valida\u00e7\u00e3o de demanda; sem multi-rede | POE gera o link server-side (`?tag=` cru), integra ao funil de valida\u00e7\u00e3o e ao criativo, multi-rede |\n| **Plugins AAWP / AzonPress** (WordPress) | Automa\u00e7\u00e3o de links + tabelas Amazon | Ricos em display; caches de produto | Presos ao WordPress; dependiam de PA-API (agora morta \u2192 Creators API); pagos; sem funil de valida\u00e7\u00e3o | POE n\u00e3o depende de API para o link; \u00e9 per-user multi-rede + funil integrado, custo-0-first |\n| **SellTheTrend / Ecomhunt / Glimpse** | Valida\u00e7\u00e3o de produto vencedor | Dados agregados de tend\u00eancia | **Pagos** (violam a diretiva anti-custo); caixa-preta; n\u00e3o geram o link nem o criativo | POE valida com sinais **gratuitos** (Trends/Meta Ad Library/Reddit/SERP) com **converg\u00eancia \u22652 + fontes citadas**, e fecha o loop at\u00e9 o criativo |\n\n---\n\n## 5. Market Opportunity & Timing\n\n**Por que agora \u2014 tr\u00eas janelas alinhadas:**\n1. **Mudan\u00e7a regulat\u00f3ria de API (Amazon):** a PA-API 5.0 foi **desligada** (depreca\u00e7\u00e3o 15/05/2026, prazo estendido 30/06/2026 \u2014 j\u00e1 no passado em 2026-07-23). Qualquer integra\u00e7\u00e3o escrita sobre ela nasce morta. O achado material \u2014 **o link b\u00e1sico Amazon dispensa API** (string-append `?tag=` sale-attributable) \u2014 abre uma via mais simples E mais barata que o rail ML j\u00e1 provado. Fonte: [Amazon PA-API docs](https://webservices.amazon.com/paapi5/documentation/), [Amazon Program Policies](https://affiliate-program.amazon.com/help/operating/policies).\n2. **Maturidade de rail interno:** o MCORCH **j\u00e1 tem** o rail per-user do ML materialmente vivo (`affiliate_config` + `vm_affiliate_products` + `_shared/affiliate.ts`). Generalizar para N redes \u00e9 mecanicamente pequeno (enum + coluna + dispatcher) \u2014 o custo de oportunidade de N\u00c3O fazer \u00e9 alto.\n3. **Comportamento de mercado:** valida\u00e7\u00e3o-antes-de-investir virou norma entre operadores maduros (\"enough evidence to decide, not perfect certainty\" \u2014 [Maze](https://maze.co/blog/market-validation/)); o stack gratuito de sinais existe mas ningu\u00e9m o orquestra com converg\u00eancia honesta.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Market Requirements\n\n> Priority: **Critical** (must have at launch) | **High** | **Medium** | **Low**\n\n| ID | Market Requirement | Priority | Rationale | Source |\n|----|-------------------|----------|-----------|--------|\n| MR-POE-001 | Gerar o link de afiliado **do pr\u00f3prio usu\u00e1rio** a partir de um link de produto original, **multi-rede** (ML + Amazon + N), com atribui\u00e7\u00e3o de receita correta **por tenant** | Critical | Requisito central do Sovereign; rail ML j\u00e1 provado; atribui\u00e7\u00e3o cruzada = fraude por design | Blueprint \u00a71, \u00a76; API Tenancy Model |\n| MR-POE-002 | Conformidade **ToS Amazon**: sem scraping da Amazon, sem cloaking/redirect, tag s\u00f3 do pr\u00f3prio usu\u00e1rio | Critical | Violar = **ban de conta afiliada** (blast radius do Owner) | Blueprint \u00a72 Pilar I; [Amazon Program Policies](https://affiliate-program.amazon.com/help/operating/policies) |\n| MR-POE-003 | **Login em portal de afiliado em runtime sem persistir credencial** (sess\u00e3o ef\u00eamera, zero disco) | Critical | Diretiva \"solicita login com seguran\u00e7a sem armazenar nada\"; Pernas de IA cobram via `deduct_mco_coins` (4\u00d7-floor, `docs/processes/mcoin-cost-calibration.md`). **Valores indicativos \u2014 calibrar por OTD-POE-010 antes de fixar `COIN_COSTS`.**\n\n| Operation | Coins/Run (indicativo) | Plan Tier Mapping | Monthly Volume (est.) | Notes |\n|-----------|-----------|-------------------|-----------------------|-------|\n| `resolve-affiliate-link` (colar link \u2192 link) | **0** | Todos | Alto | String-append; sem IA |\n| `validate-opportunity` G1\u2013G3 (sinais gratuitos) | **0** | Todos | M\u00e9dio | Fail-soft; sem IA obrigat\u00f3ria |\n| G3 persona synthesis (IA) | ~1 (calibrar) | Starter(500)/Pro(2000)/Ent(10k) | M\u00e9dio | Reusa motor de gera\u00e7\u00e3o |\n| G4 avatar (Spaces char-ref Nano Banana) | ~9 (calibrar, ~US$0.04) | \" | Baixo-m\u00e9dio | Reusa `avatarIdentity`/`characterReference` |\n| G5 conte\u00fado (`orchestrate-content`) | 10 (existente) | \" | Baixo-m\u00e9dio | Self-bill do pipeline existente |\n| Creators API call (Fatia 4) | A medir | \" | Baixo | S\u00f3 com gate 10 vendas/30d |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Constraints & Assumptions\n\n### Constraints\n- **PA-API 5.0 \u00e9 proibida** no design (morta). Amazon = string-append `?tag=` ou Creators API (Fatia 4).\n- **Amazon = sem `/go` redirect** (cloaking); sem scraping server-side; tag s\u00f3 do pr\u00f3prio user.\n- **Sinais gratuitos apenas** \u2014 Apify pago proibido; custo USD externo dos sinais = 0.\n- **Login em portal s\u00f3 no runtime residencial do usu\u00e1rio** (IP datacenter bloqueado); zero-persist.\n- **C\u00f3digo bloqueado** at\u00e9 BoK 9/9 + `/security-review` por migration + GO Sovereign + revis\u00e3o jur\u00eddica Amazon.\n\n### Assumptions\n- O Sovereign est\u00e1 aprovado como Amazon Associate (dado da diretiva). **Ter \u226510 vendas/30d \u00e9 n\u00e3o-verificado** (OTD-POE-003).\n- O rail ML per-user (`affiliate_config`/`vm_affiliate_products`/`_shared/affiliate.ts`) permanece o molde v\u00e1lido a generalizar.\n- O ABO residencial (`authenticated-browser-operator`) suporta um perfil `isolated ephemeral`/zero-persist (a verificar \u2014 OTD-POE-005).\n- Fontes gratuitas t\u00eam cobertura suficiente para converg\u00eancia \u22652 na maioria dos nichos (fail-soft quando n\u00e3o).\n\n---\n\n## 8. Business Risk Register\n\n| Risk ID | Description | Probability | Impact | RPN | Mitigation |\n|---------|-------------|-------------|--------|-----|------------|\n| BR-RISK-001 | Ban de conta afiliada Amazon por cloaking/scraping (FM-POE-02/03) | 3 | 5 | 15 | Flag `allowsRedirectCloaking=false`; valida\u00e7\u00e3o fora da Amazon; revis\u00e3o jur\u00eddica gate |\n| BR-RISK-002 | Conector nasce morto por assumir PA-API 5.0 (FM-POE-01) | 2 | 5 | 10 | Banir \"PA-API 5.0\"; string-append primeiro; Creators API s\u00f3 Fatia 4 |\n| BR-RISK-003 | Vazamento de receita/quota cross-tenant por credencial global (FM-POE-08) | 2 | 5 | 10 | API Tenancy per-user fail-closed; sem env global user-facing |\n| BR-RISK-004 | Falso-verde de demanda (spike sazonal) \u2192 user investe em produto morto (FM-POE-07) | 4 | 3 | 12 | Converg\u00eancia \u22652 + cross-check estrutural\u00d7c\u00edclico; janela \u226512m |\n| BR-RISK-005 | Credencial viva vaza em memory dump apesar de zero-persist em disco (FM-POE-05) | 3 | 4 | 12 | Usu\u00e1rio digita a pr\u00f3pria senha (agente nunca a recebe); isolated ephemeral; consent log |\n| BR-RISK-006 | Fonte gratuita fora do ar / CAPTCHA \u2192 falso-negativo silencioso (FM-POE-06) | 4 | 3 | 12 | Fail-soft + `degraded` telemetria; nunca 1 sinal; human-paced |\n| BR-RISK-007 | Sinais scrapeados contaminam malha/spine sem proveni\u00eancia (FM-POE-09) | 3 | 3 | 9 | Cache ef\u00eamero isolado + retention clock; `content_strategy`-like persistente; nunca system nodes |\n\n> Probability & Impact: 1=Low, 5=High.   "content": "# PRD \u2014 Product Requirements Document\n\n**Project:** `product-opportunity-engine`\n**Version:** v0.1 | **Date:** 2026-07-23\n**BoK Applied:** ProdBOK + CXBOK\n**Status:** Sealed\n\n---\n\n## 1. Product Vision Statement\n\n> Para o **afiliado/criador** que precisa saber se um produto vale o investimento **e** gerar o pr\u00f3prio link compliant em cada rede, o **Product Opportunity Engine** \u00e9 um **motor de valida\u00e7\u00e3o + monetiza\u00e7\u00e3o per-user** que qualifica a oportunidade com sinais gratuitos convergentes e devolve o link de afiliado dele (ML e Amazon) mais persona, avatar e criativo \u2014 tudo num workspace reutiliz\u00e1vel. Diferente de SellTheTrend (pago) ou do SiteStripe manual (um produto por vez, sem valida\u00e7\u00e3o), nosso produto **funde validar \u2192 gerar link \u2192 gerar criativo** num s\u00f3 flywheel, custo-USD-zero-first, e **compliant por constru\u00e7\u00e3o** (anti-cloaking/anti-scraping por rede).\n\n---\n\n## 2. User Personas\n\n### Persona 1 \u2014 \"Rafael, o Afiliado Solo\" (Primary)\n\n| Attribute | Detail |\n|-----------|--------|\n| Role | Criador/afiliado solo que j\u00e1 monetiza Mercado Livre e quer somar Amazon |\n| Goals | N\u00e3o perder tempo/dinheiro em produto sem demanda; gerar o pr\u00f3prio link Amazon sem risco de ban; produzir criativo r\u00e1pido |\n| Frustrations | Ferramentas de valida\u00e7\u00e3o s\u00e3o pagas; n\u00e3o sabe que o redirect pr\u00f3prio viola o ToS Amazon; gera link manual no SiteStripe |\n| Tech-savviness | 3/5 (usa a plataforma, n\u00e3o l\u00ea ToS de API) |\n| MCORCH usage pattern | Cola link \u2192 gera link do dele; roda o funil sobre um nicho; empacota workspace |\n| Quote | _\"S\u00f3 quero saber se vende antes de gravar 10 v\u00eddeos \u2014 e o link tem que ser o MEU, sem tomar ban.\"_ |\n\n### Persona 2 \u2014 \"Marina, a Operadora Multi-Produto\" (Secondary)\n\n| Attribute | Detail |\n|-----------|--------|\n| Role | Operadora que roda N campanhas por produto/rede e precisa de atribui\u00e7\u00e3o correta |\n| Goals | Atribui\u00e7\u00e3o de receita correta por produto e por rede; escalar sem misturar quota/receita entre contas |\n| Frustrations | Rails que misturam per-user com global; contar clique/venda difere por rede e ningu\u00e9m modela isso |\n| Tech-savviness | 4/5 |\n| MCORCH usage pattern | Multi-rede; dados de produto (Creators API quando o gate permite); dashboard de converg\u00eancia |\n| Quote | _\"Cada rede tem uma regra de cookie e de tag. Eu preciso que o sistema saiba disso, n\u00e3o eu.\"_ |\n\n---\n\n## 3. User Journey Maps\n\n### Journey: \"Validar e Monetizar um Nicho\" (Rafael, Happy Path)\n\n| Stage | Action | Touchpoint | Emotion | Opportunity |\n|-------|--------|-----------|---------|-------------|\n| Awareness | Tem uma ideia de nicho/produto | `/dashboard` | \ud83d\ude10 Neutral | Sugerir \"valide antes de investir\" |\n| Consideration | Roda o funil de valida\u00e7\u00e3o sobre o nicho | Painel do funil (G1\u2013G3) | \ud83e\udd14 Curious | Veredito material com fontes citadas |\n| Activation | V\u00ea \"aprovado com 2 sinais convergentes\" e cola o link do produto | \"Colar link \u2192 link do usu\u00e1rio\" | \ud83d\ude0a Excited | Link do dele instant\u00e2neo, compliant |\n| Value | Gera persona \u2192 avatar \u2192 criativo (rascunho/privado) | G4\u2013G5 (Spaces + orchestrate-content) | \ud83d\ude80 Delighted | Criativo pronto sem investir cego |\n| Retention | Empacota tudo num workspace reutiliz\u00e1vel | G6 (Spaces canvas + malha) | \u2764\ufe0f Loyal | Volta para o pr\u00f3ximo nicho |\n\n#### Edge Case 1: Oportunidade reprovada (ru\u00eddo sazonal)\n> O funil detecta 1 spike c\u00edclico sem converg\u00eancia estrutural (Movers&Shakers sem Trends \u226512m). Veredito: **\"rejeitada \u2014 prov\u00e1vel sazonalidade\"**, com o cross-check mostrado. Nenhum mcoCoin gasto. Rafael pivota barato.\n\n#### Edge Case 2: Amazon n\u00e3o configurado\n> Rafael cola um link Amazon sem ter informado o Tracking ID. Resposta **402 `amazon_not_configured`** com CTA \"Configure seu Amazon em /dashboard/settings\". Nunca devolve um link com tag global/de terceiro (own-tag-only).\n\n#### Edge Case 3: Fonte gratuita fora do ar\n> Google Trends retorna CAPTCHA/429. O gate n\u00e3o crasha: marca o sinal como `degraded`, exige o segundo sinal de outra fonte, e o veredito honra a aus\u00eancia (\"1/2 sinais \u2014 inconclusivo, tente novamente\"). Empty-state honesto, n\u00e3o erro.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Feature Inventory (MoSCoW)\n\n> Effort: S=hours, M=days, L=week, XL=sprint\n\n### Must Have (MVP \u2014 Fatia 1 + Fatia 2)\n\n| ID | Feature | Persona | BR Traced | Effort | mcoCoins/run | Notes |\n|----|---------|---------|-----------|--------|-------------|-------|\n| PR-POE-001 | **Colar link \u2192 gerar link de afiliado (multi-rede)** | P1, P2 | BR-POE-001, BR-POE-005 | M | 0 | Detecta rede pela URL; ML=`meli.la`/`matt_word`, Amazon=`?tag=` cru |\n| PR-POE-002 | **Dispatcher multi-rede** `buildAffiliateUrl(network,\u2026)` com contrato por rede | P2 | BR-POE-005 | M | 0 | Data-driven (param/cookie/`allowsRedirectCloaking`), n\u00e3o `if network` |\n| PR-POE-003 | **Guarda de conformidade Amazon** (sem `/go`, sem scraping, own-tag) | P1 | BR-POE-002 | S | 0 | `allowsRedirectCloaking=false`; valida host `amazon.*`; tag per-user |\n| PR-POE-004 | **Conectar Amazon (Settings)** \u2014 digitar/validar Tracking ID (BYOK) | P1 | BR-POE-010 | S | 0 | Fail-closed 402 quando ausente; funciona sem Creators API |\n| PR-POE-005 | **Funil de valida\u00e7\u00e3o G1\u2013G3** (sinais gratuitos + converg\u00eancia \u22652) | P1, P2 | BR-POE-004, BR-POE-008 | L | 0 | Fail-soft; veredito material + fontes; n\u00f3 `observation` |\n| PR-POE-006 | **Proveni\u00eancia/reten\u00e7\u00e3o dos sinais** (ef\u00eamero isolado) | P1 | BR-POE-006 | M | 0 | `retention_expires_at`; nunca system nodes nem spine |\n\n### Should Have (v1.0 \u2014 Fatia 3)\n\n| ID | Feature | Persona | BR Traced | Effort | mcoCoins/run | Notes |\n|----|---------|---------|-----------|--------|-------------|-------|\n| PR-POE-007 | **G4 Avatar** da persona (Spaces `avatarIdentity`/`characterReference`) | P1 | BR-POE-009 | M | ~9 (calibrar) | Char-ref sem-treino default Nano Banana |\n| PR-POE-008 | **G5 Conte\u00fado** nativo por canal (`orchestrate-content`) + `creative_metrics.hook_rate` | P1 | BR-POE-009, BR-POE-007 | M | 10 | Privado-primeiro (rascunho) |\n| PR-POE-009 | **G6 Workspace** de oportunidade (Spaces canvas reutiliz\u00e1vel) | P1, P2 | BR-POE-009 | M | 0\u20131 | Consolida nicho+produto+persona+avatar+conte\u00fado+link |\n| PR-POE-010 | **Login ef\u00eamero de portal** (ABO zero-persist) \u2014 extrai Tracking ID / `amzn.to` | P1 | BR-POE-003 | L | 0 | Runtime residencial; `context.close()` descarta tudo |\n\n### Could Have (Future \u2014 Fatia 4, gated)\n\n| ID | Feature | Persona | BR Traced | Effort | mcoCoins/run | Notes |\n|----|---------|---------|-----------|--------|-------------|-------|\n| PR-POE-011 | **Creators API** (OAuth2 LwA per-user) para dados de produto Amazon | P2 | BR-POE-010 | XL | a medir | S\u00f3 com gate 10 vendas/30d; degrade gracioso |\n| PR-POE-012 | **N-\u00e9sima rede** de afiliado (3\u00aa rede) via dispatcher | P2 | BR-POE-005 | M | 0 | Adiciona linha ao contrato por rede |\n| PR-POE-013 | **Meta Ad Library / Reddit / SERP** como fontes automatizadas ToS-safe | P1 | BR-POE-004 | L | 0 | S\u00f3 fontes com automa\u00e7\u00e3o permitida (OTD-POE-007) |\n\n### Won't Have (This Cycle)\n\n- Scraping server-side de datacenter contra a Amazon (**proibido por ToS + IP bloqueado** \u2014 nunca).\n- `pytrends` em produ\u00e7\u00e3o sem fail-soft (fr\u00e1gil/ToS-cinza).\n- Qualquer provider de sinais **pago** (Apify e similares \u2014 diretiva anti-custo).\n- Persistir credencial de afiliado no Vault/disco a partir do login em runtime.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Release Phasing\n\n### MVP Scope (Fatia 1 + Fatia 2)\n**Included:** PR-POE-001..006\n**Success gate:** Colar link Amazon real \u2192 200 com `?tag=` do user (0 `/go`); funil sobre 1 nicho \u2192 veredito com \u22652 fontes + n\u00f3 na malha por `SELECT`. Toda migration `/security-review` SAFE.\n\n### v1.0 Scope (Fatia 3)\n**Adds:** PR-POE-007..010\n**Success gate:** Login residencial extrai Tracking ID/`amzn.to`, `context.close()` deixa profile dir vazio (`ls`); criativo com `hook_rate`; workspace G6 empacotado; E2E UI audit 1920\u00d71080 se houver UI nova.\n\n### Future Scope (Fatia 4 \u2014 gated)\n**Items:** PR-POE-011..013\n**Condition:** GO Sovereign + gate de 10 vendas/30d verificado (OTD-POE-003) + contrato Creators API fetchado (OTD-POE-001).\n\n---\n\n## 6. Luxury UX Specifications\n\n### 6.1 Color & Visual Identity\n\n> Herda tokens MIV (`reference_miv_design_tokens`): `--accent` = cyan; nebula s\u00f3 em superf\u00edcies de mem\u00f3ria; gold s\u00f3 em valor (comiss\u00e3o/receita). Void como base.\n\n| Token | Value | Usage |\n|-------|-------|-------|\n| `--bg-deep` | void (base MIV) | Canvas background |\n| `--accent` | cyan | CTA prim\u00e1rio, estados ativos, \"colar link\" |\n| gold | (classe expl\u00edcita) | Sinal de VALOR \u2014 comiss\u00e3o estimada, veredito \"aprovado\" |\n| nebula | (classe expl\u00edcita) | S\u00f3 n\u00f3 de mem\u00f3ria (workspace na malha) |\n| `--glass-surface` | `rgba(255,255,255,0.04)` | Cards do funil |\n\n### 6.2 Animation Guidelines\n\n| Element | Library | Duration | Easing |\n|---------|---------|----------|--------|\n| Transi\u00e7\u00e3o do funil G1\u2192G6 | Framer Motion | 300ms | `easeInOut` |\n| Pulse \"sinal chegando\" (fail-soft) | CSS keyframes | 2s | `ease-in-out infinite` |\n| Veredito reveal (aprovado/reprovado) | Framer Motion | 400ms | `easeOut` |\n| Micro-intera\u00e7\u00f5es (copiar link) | Framer Motion | 150ms | `easeOut` |\n\n### 6.3 Interaction Patterns\n\n- **Veredito honesto:** aprovado = gold glow + fontes citadas clic\u00e1veis; reprovado = neutro + raz\u00e3o (\"ru\u00eddo sazonal\"); inconclusivo = \"1/2 sinais\" sem falso-verde.\n- **Empty-states honestos:** fonte fora do ar \u2192 `degraded` vis\u00edvel (\"Google Trends indispon\u00edvel \u2014 usando Meta Ad Library\"), **nunca** um erro cru ou um verde falso.\n- **Copiar link:** bot\u00e3o \u00fanico; toast PT-BR \"Link de afiliado copiado\" (cyan); mostra a rede detectada e a tag usada (mascarada).\n- **Gate Creators API comunicado sem bloquear:** banner informativo \"Dados de produto Amazon exigem 10 vendas/30d \u2014 a gera\u00e7\u00e3o de link funciona sem isso\".\n\n### 6.4 Accessibility (WCAG 2.1 AA)\n\n- Contraste \u2265 4.5:1 (aten\u00e7\u00e3o ao gold sobre void \u2014 validar com senior-rubric).\n- Fontes citadas do veredito s\u00e3o links naveg\u00e1veis por teclado.\n- ARIA labels nos bot\u00f5es de copiar/gerar link.\n- Foco vis\u00edvel \u2265 2px.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Acceptance Criteria (Gherkin)\n\n```gherkin\nFeature: Product Opportunity Engine\n\n  Scenario: Paste an Amazon product link and get my own affiliate link (PR-POE-001)\n    Given I am authenticated and have configured my Amazon Tracking ID\n    When I paste a genuine amazon.* product URL\n    Then the response is HTTP 200 with the same product URL carrying ?tag=<my Tracking ID>\n    And the link is NOT routed through /go (no cloaking)\n    And no server-side fetch is made against amazon.*\n\n  Scenario: Amazon not configured is fail-closed (PR-POE-004)\n    Given I am authenticated and have NOT configured my Amazon Tracking ID\n    When I paste an amazon.* product URL\n    Then the response is HTTP 402 with body { error: \"amazon_not_configured\", action: \"Configure your Amazon at /dashboard/settings\" }\n    And no link with a global or third-party tag is ever returned\n\n  Scenario: Validation funnel approves only on signal convergence (PR-POE-005)\n    Given I run the funnel on a niche\n    When fewer than 2 independent free signals converge\n    Then the verdict is \"inconclusive\" or \"rejected\" and NO mcoCoins are spent\n    When at least 2 independent signals converge (e.g. Google Trends >=12m AND Meta Ad Library active advertisers)\n    Then the verdict is \"approved\" with the source URLs cited\n    And an observation node is inserted into the Knowledge Mesh\n\n  Scenario: Ephemeral portal login persists nothing (PR-POE-010)\n    Given the residential runner opens the affiliate portal\n    When I authenticate in my own browser and the agent extracts the Tracking ID\n    Then context.close() leaves the profile directory empty on disk\n    And only the non-secret artifact (Tracking ID / amzn.to) is stored per-user\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Traceability Matrix\n\n| MR ID | BR ID | PR ID |\n|-------|-------|-------|\n| MR-POE-001 | BR-POE-001, BR-POE-005 | PR-POE-001, PR-POE-002 |\n| MR-POE-002 | BR-POE-002 | PR-POE-003 |\n| MR-POE-003 | BR-POE-003 | PR-POE-010 |\n| MR-POE-004 | BR-POE-008 | PR-POE-005 |\n| MR-POE-005 | BR-POE-004 | PR-POE-005, PR-POE-013 |\n| MR-POE-008 | BR-POE-009 | PR-POE-007, PR-POE-008, PR-POE-009 |\n| MR-POE-009 | BR-POE-007 | PR-POE-008 |\n| MR-POE-010 | BR-POE-010 | PR-POE-004, PR-POE-011 |\n| MR-POE-011 | BR-POE-006 | PR-POE-006 |\n\n---\n\n_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0_\n"

Non-Functional Requirements\n\n| ID | Category | Requirement | Target | Measurement |\n|----|----------|-------------|--------|-------------|\n| NFR-POE-001 | Performance | `resolve-affiliate-link` p95 (string-append, sem IA) | < 400ms | Supabase logs |\n| NFR-POE-002 | Performance | Funil G1\u2013G3 (sinais gratuitos, async) \u2014 tempo at\u00e9 veredito | < 60s p95 (async job) | Job telemetry |\n| NFR-POE-003 | Security | Todo edge fn user-facing exige JWT v\u00e1lido | 100% | `/security-review` |\n| NFR-POE-004 | Security | Credencial de afiliado resolvida **per-user fail-closed**; 0 env global user-facing | 100% | Auditoria de c\u00f3digo + smoke |\n| NFR-POE-005 | Compliance | **0** `/go` no caminho Amazon; **0** fetch server-side contra `amazon.*` | 100% | `grep` + revis\u00e3o de c\u00f3digo |\n| NFR-POE-006 | Privacy | Login em runtime deixa **0 bytes** de sess\u00e3o em disco | 100% | `ls` do profile dir p\u00f3s-`context.close()` |\n| NFR-POE-007 | Reliability | Fail-soft coverage das fontes gratuitas | 100% dos caminhos de fonte | Smoke: fonte fora do ar \u2192 `degraded`, n\u00e3o crash |\n| NFR-POE-008 | Data quality | Todo sinal persistido carrega origem+licen\u00e7a+`retention_expires_at`; 0 em system nodes/spine | 100% | Constraint + smoke |\n| NFR-POE-009 | Cost | Custo USD externo dos sinais | US$ 0 | Auditoria de provider no caminho |\n| NFR-POE-010 | Maintainability | Adicionar N-\u00e9sima rede = 1 valor de enum + 1 linha de contrato (sem tocar o dispatcher) | \u2264 1 migration | Revis\u00e3o de PR |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Interface Requirements\n\n### 4.1 UI \u2192 Hook \u2192 Edge Function Contract\n\n```\n[Component: PasteAffiliateLinkCard]            [Component: OpportunityFunnelPanel]\n  \u2193 calls                                          \u2193 calls\n[Hook: useAffiliateResolve]                     [Hook: useOpportunityFunnel]\n  \u2193 POST JWT                                       \u2193 POST JWT (async 202 \u2192 poll)\n[EdgeFn: resolve-affiliate-link]                [EdgeFn: validate-opportunity (+ -poll)]\n  \u2193 reads (per-user, fail-closed)                  \u2193 reads/writes\n[affiliate_config, affiliate_network_contract]  [product_opportunities, opportunity_signals, mcorch_nodes]\n  \u2193 via SharedLib\n[_shared/affiliate.ts \u2192 buildAffiliateUrl(network,\u2026)]\n```\n\n### 4.2 External API Contracts\n\n#### `POST /functions/v1/resolve-affiliate-link` (FR-POE-001/002/003, Fatia 1)\n\n**Auth:** Bearer JWT (user scope)\n**Request:**\n```typescript\n{\n  product_url: string   // link de produto ORIGINAL (sem UTM), ML ou Amazon\n  network?: 'mercadolivre' | 'amazon'   // opcional; se ausente, detecta pela URL\n}\n```\n**Response (200):**\n```typescript\n{\n  status: 'ok'\n  network: 'mercadolivre' | 'amazon'\n  affiliate_url: string   // ML: meli.la/matt_word ; Amazon: <product_url>?tag=<amazon_tag>  (CRU, sem /go)\n  attribution: 'sale' | 'click'   // Amazon='sale' (cookie 24h+90d) ; ML bare='click', signed='sale'\n  cloaked: false                  // Amazon SEMPRE false (anti-cloaking)\n}\n```\n**Error codes:**\n| Code | Condition |\n|------|-----------|\n| 401 | Missing or invalid JWT |\n| 402 | Credencial per-user ausente \u2192 `{ error: \"<network>_not_configured\", action: \"\u2026/dashboard/settings\" }` |\n| 422 | URL inv\u00e1lida / host n\u00e3o reconhecido / n\u00e3o \u00e9 produto genu\u00edno |\n| 500 | Internal error (logged to infra_health_logs) |\n\n#### `POST /functions/v1/validate-opportunity` (FR-POE-004/006/016, Fatia 2, async)\n\n**Auth:** Bearer JWT (user scope)\n**Request:**\n```typescript\n{\n  niche?: string              // texto do nicho, OU\n  product_url?: string        // link de produto para G2\n  gates?: ('G1'|'G2'|'G3'|'G4'|'G5'|'G6')[]   // default G1..G3\n}\n```\n**Response (202 Accepted):**\n```typescript\n{ status: 'queued', opportunity_id: string, poll: 'poll://validate-opportunity-poll' }\n```\nPoll (`validate-opportunity-poll`) retorna estado terminal:\n```typescript\n{\n  status: 'approved' | 'rejected' | 'inconclusive'\n  gates: { id: string, passed: boolean, signals: { source: string, url: string, value: string, degraded: boolean }[] }[]\n  verdict_text: string        // PT-BR, honesto\n  node_id?: string            // mcorch_nodes observation UUID (quando aprovado)\n}\n```\n**Error codes:** 401 (JWT) \u00b7 402 (mcoCoins de perna de IA quando G4+ solicitado) \u00b7 422 (nem niche nem product_url) \u00b7 500.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Data Requirements\n\n| Field | Type | Nullable | Validation Rule | Error Message (PT-BR) |\n|-------|------|----------|-----------------|----------------------|\n| `product_url` | `text` | NOT NULL | URL v\u00e1lida; host \u2208 allowlist da rede (`affiliate_network_contract.host_allowlist`); \u00e9 URL de produto genu\u00edna | \"Cole um link de produto v\u00e1lido do Mercado Livre ou da Amazon.\" |\n| `amazon_tag` (`affiliate_config`) | `text` | NULL | Formato Tracking ID Amazon (`^[a-z0-9-]{1,20}-2[0-9]$` aprox.; validar no ingest \u2014 OTD) | \"Tracking ID da Amazon inv\u00e1lido.\" |\n| `network` | `vm_affiliate_network` (enum) | NOT NULL | \u2208 {`mercadolivre`, `mercadopago`, `amazon`} | \"Rede de afiliado n\u00e3o suportada.\" |\n| `niche` | `text` | NULL (mas niche OU product_url obrigat\u00f3rio) | 3..200 chars | \"Descreva o nicho ou cole um link de produto.\" |\n| `signal.source` | `text` | NOT NULL | \u2208 allowlist de fontes gratuitas ToS-safe (OTD-POE-007) | \"Fonte de sinal n\u00e3o permitida.\" |\n| `retention_expires_at` | `timestamptz` | NOT NULL | > now(); default now()+30d | (interno) |\n\n---\n\n## 6. Test Strategy Matrix\n\n| FR ID | Unit Test | Integration Test | E2E Test | Notes |\n|-------|-----------|-----------------|----------|-------|\n| FR-POE-001 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2014 | `buildAffiliateUrl` por rede; contrato lido |\n| FR-POE-002 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | **Gate:** `?tag=` presente, 0 `/go`, host amazon validado |\n| FR-POE-003 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2014 | Fail-closed 402 (throwaway sem config) |\n| FR-POE-004 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | Converg\u00eancia \u22652; cross-check; kill-rate |\n| FR-POE-005 | \u2014 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | Zero-persist: `ls` profile vazio; senha nunca ao servidor |\n| FR-POE-006 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2014 | N\u00f3 `observation` com UUID; 0 sinal em system nodes/spine |\n| FR-POE-008 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2014 | Atribui\u00e7\u00e3o por rede (clique ML / venda Amazon) |\n| FR-POE-013 | \u2705 Required | \u2014 | \u2014 | Adicionar rede = 1 linha; dispatcher inalterado |\n| FR-POE-016 | \u2705 Required | \u2705 Required | \u2014 | Fonte fora do ar \u2192 `degraded`, n\u00e3o crash; telemetria |\n\n**Test files location (a criar sob gate de c\u00f3digo):**\n- Smoke (LIVE, zero-cost, herm\u00e9ticos): `scripts/qa/smoke-affiliate-dispatcher.ts`, `scripts/qa/smoke-amazon-tag-append.ts`, `scripts/qa/smoke-opportunity-funnel.ts`, `scripts/qa/smoke-ephemeral-login.ts`\n- Unit: `supabase/functions/_shared/affiliate.test.ts` (extend), `src/hooks/__tests__/useAffiliateResolve.test.ts`\n- E2E: reuso do `e2e-user-zero` + audit UI 1920\u00d71080 (Canvas/Settings)\n\n---\n\n## 7. IA por perna se G4+ | per-user signal keys (OTD-POE-007) |\n| `validate-opportunity-poll` | `validate-opportunity-poll/index.ts` | JWT (user) | \u2014 | none |\n| `amazon-creators-proxy` *(Fatia 4, gated)* | `amazon-creators-proxy/index.ts` | JWT (user) | por chamada | per-user `amazon_creators_secret` (Vault) |\n\n> **Reuso (n\u00e3o novo):** `orchestrate-content` (G5), `embed-mcorch-node` (n\u00f3s), `process-affiliate-link` (**s\u00f3 ML** \u2014 nunca Amazon), Spaces `canvas-execute` (G4 avatar).\n\n### 2.4 Shared Library Extension (`supabase/functions/_shared/affiliate.ts`)\n\n| Symbol | Kind | Contract |\n|--------|------|----------|\n| `NETWORK_CONTRACTS` | const registry | Espelho em c\u00f3digo do `affiliate_network_contract` (SSOT do dispatcher); `{ [network]: { tagParam, cookieWindow, allowsRedirectCloaking, hostAllowlist, dataApi } }` |\n| `buildAffiliateUrl` | fn | `(admin, network, external_id, ownerCreds, ownerId) \u2192 string`; roteia por rede; ML \u2192 `buildMlProductUrl` (existente); amazon \u2192 `buildAmazonProductUrl` |\n| `buildAmazonProductUrl` | fn | `(productUrl, amazonTag) \u2192 string`; valida host `amazon.*`, remove `tag` pr\u00e9-existente, anexa `?tag=`; **nunca** envolve `/go`; retorna cru |\n| `detectNetwork` | fn | `(url) \u2192 network \\| null` por host allowlist |\n\n### 2.5 New DB Objects\n\n| Object | Type | Purpose | Migration file (stub) |\n|--------|------|---------|----------------------|\n| `vm_affiliate_network += 'amazon'` | enum value | Habilita cat\u00e1logo/dispatcher Amazon | `<ts>_poe_amazon_network_enum.sql` |\n| `affiliate_config.amazon_tag` | column | Tracking ID Amazon per-user (BYOK, fail-closed) | `<ts>_poe_affiliate_config_amazon_tag.sql` |\n| `affiliate_network_contract` | table (reference/system) | Contrato por rede data-driven (param/cookie/cloaking/host/API) | `<ts>_poe_affiliate_network_contract.sql` |\n| `product_opportunities` | table (per-user) | Verdade persistente do funil (verdito, gates, embedding) | `<ts>_poe_product_opportunities.sql` |\n| `opportunity_signals` | table (per-user, ephemeral) | Sinais scrapeados com proveni\u00eancia + retention clock | `<ts>_poe_opportunity_signals.sql` |\n| `amazon_creators_credentials` *(Fatia 4, gated)* | table (per-user, Vault) | OAuth2 LwA Credential ID/Secret cifrado | `<ts>_poe_amazon_creators_credentials.sql` |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Data Flow Diagrams\n\n### Use Case A: Colar link Amazon \u2192 link do usu\u00e1rio (FR-POE-001/002/003 \u00b7 s\u00edncrono)\n\n```\nUser            useAffiliateResolve      resolve-affiliate-link      DB / SharedLib\n \u2502                   \u2502                          \u2502                         \u2502\n \u2502\u2500[paste amazon URL]\u25b6                          \u2502                         \u2502\n \u2502                   \u2502\u2500[POST JWT {product_url}]\u2500\u25b6                         \u2502\n \u2502                   \u2502                          \u2502\u2500[detectNetwork]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502 affiliate_network_contract\n \u2502                   \u2502                          \u2502\u2500[SELECT amazon_tag]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502 affiliate_config (user=auth.uid())\n \u2502                   \u2502                          \u2502  (ausente \u2192 402 fail-closed, own-tag-only)\n \u2502                   \u2502                          \u2502\u2500[buildAmazonProductUrl]\u25b6\u2502 append ?tag=, strip old tag, NO /go\n \u2502                   \u2502\u25c0\u2500[200 {affiliate_url, cloaked:false, attribution:'sale'}]\n \u2502\u25c0\u2500[copy link + rede + tag mascarada]          \u2502                         \u2502\n```\n**Nenhum fetch server-side contra amazon.*** (FR-POE-002 \u00b7 NFR-POE-005). String-append puro.\n\n### Use Case B: Funil de valida\u00e7\u00e3o G1\u2013G3 (FR-POE-004/006/016 \u00b7 async)\n\n```\nUser      useOpportunityFunnel    validate-opportunity     free signals (fail-soft)    DB\n \u2502             \u2502                        \u2502                        \u2502                       \u2502\n \u2502\u2500[niche]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502                        \u2502                        \u2502                       \u2502\n \u2502             \u2502\u2500[POST JWT {niche}]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502                        \u2502                       \u2502\n \u2502             \u2502\u25c0\u2500[202 {opportunity_id}]\u2502\u2500[INSERT queued]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u253c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502 product_opportunities\n \u2502             \u2502                        \u2502\u2500[G1 Trends+MetaAdLib]\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502 (degraded\u2192telemetry)  \u2502\n \u2502             \u2502                        \u2502\u2500[G2 Movers\u00d7Trends xcheck]\u25b6 (Creators API OR residential; never datacenter\u2192amazon)\n \u2502             \u2502                        \u2502\u2500[G3 review/Reddit mining]\u25b6\u2502                      \u2502\n \u2502             \u2502                        \u2502\u2500[converge \u22652? verdict]  \u2502                       \u2502\n \u2502             \u2502                        \u2502\u2500[INSERT signals + retention]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502 opportunity_signals\n \u2502             \u2502                        \u2502\u2500[embed observation node]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502 mcorch_nodes\n \u2502             \u2502\u2500[poll]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502 validate-opportunity-poll \u2192 terminal verdict    \u2502\n \u2502\u25c0\u2500[verdict + cited URLs]              \u2502                        \u2502                       \u2502\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. API Contract Definitions\n\n> Todo edge fn valida JWT antes de qualquer l\u00f3gica. Contratos completos em `04-frd.md \u00a74.2`.\n\n### `POST /functions/v1/resolve-affiliate-link`\n\n```typescript\ninterface ResolveAffiliateRequest { product_url: string; network?: 'mercadolivre'|'amazon' }\ninterface ResolveAffiliateResponse {\n  status: 'ok'; network: 'mercadolivre'|'amazon';\n  affiliate_url: string; attribution: 'sale'|'click'; cloaked: false\n}\n// per-user credential resolution (fail-closed):\n// \u2192 SELECT amazon_tag FROM affiliate_config WHERE user_id = auth.uid()\n// \u2192 absent \u21d2 HTTP 402 { error: \"amazon_not_configured\", action: \".../dashboard/settings\" }\n// mcoCoins deduction point: NONE (0 \u2014 string-append)\n```\n\n### `POST /functions/v1/validate-opportunity` (async 202)\n\n```typescript\ninterface ValidateOpportunityRequest { niche?: string; product_url?: string; gates?: string[] }\ninterface ValidateOpportunityAccepted { status: 'queued'; opportunity_id: string; poll: string }\n// mcoCoins deduction point (only when G4+ AI legs requested):\n// \u2192 await supabase.rpc('deduct_mco_coins', { p_user_id, p_amount }) with refund-on-failure\n// \u2192 HTTP 402 if insufficient. Free signals (G1\u2013G3) deduct 0.\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Security Design\n\n### 5.1 Threat Model (STRIDE)\n\n| Component | Threat | Type | Severity | Mitigation |\n|-----------|--------|------|----------|------------|\n| `resolve-affiliate-link` | Acesso n\u00e3o-autenticado | Spoofing | High | JWT validation no entry point (verify_jwt / ES256 conforme SOP `edge-jwt-identity-verification`) |\n| `affiliate_config.amazon_tag` | Leitura cross-tenant da tag de outro user | Information Disclosure | Critical | RLS `user_id = auth.uid()`; select de safe-columns; tag mascarada na UI |\n| Amazon link path | **Cloaking via `/go`** (ToS violation \u2192 ban) | Tampering / Repudiation | Critical | `allowsRedirectCloaking=false`; `cloaked:false` garantido; `grep` 0 `/go` (NFR-POE-005) |\n| Amazon data path | **Scraping server-side contra amazon.*** (ToS + IP block) | Tampering | Critical | Proibido por design; valida\u00e7\u00e3o fora da Amazon; Creators API/residencial only (FM-POE-02) |\n| Tag resolution | Uso de tag global/de terceiro (own-tag-only) | Elevation / Fraud | Critical | Per-user fail-closed 402; **0** env global user-facing (FM-POE-04/08) |\n| Ephemeral login | Credencial viva vaza em memory dump | Information Disclosure | High | Usu\u00e1rio digita a pr\u00f3pria senha (agente nunca a recebe); isolated ephemeral; `context.close()`; consent log (FM-POE-05) |\n| `opportunity_signals` | Sinais de terceiros contaminam malha/spine sem proveni\u00eancia | Information Disclosure / Repudiation | Medium | Cache ef\u00eamero isolado + `retention_expires_at`; nunca system nodes/spine (FM-POE-09) |\n| mcoCoins (G4+ legs) | Bypass client-side da dedu\u00e7\u00e3o | Tampering | Critical | S\u00f3 `deduct_mco_coins` RPC server-side + refund-on-failure |\n\n### 5.2 RLS Policy Definitions\n\n```sql\n-- affiliate_config.amazon_tag: coluna aditiva na tabela per-user existente (RLS j\u00e1 own-scoped;\n--   amazon_tag \u00e9 sens\u00edvel \u2192 mesma prote\u00e7\u00e3o do matt_word: select via safe-columns na UI).\n\n-- affiliate_network_contract: REFERENCE data (system rows, user_id IS NULL), public-read, service-role write.\nALTER TABLE public.affiliate_network_contract ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nCREATE POLICY \"network_contract: public read\"\n  ON public.affiliate_network_contract FOR SELECT USING (true);   -- non-sensitive reference data\n-- INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE: nenhuma policy p/ role autenticado \u21d2 default deny (service-role only via RPC/migration).\n\n-- product_opportunities: per-user own-scoped.\nALTER TABLE public.product_opportunities ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nCREATE POLICY \"opportunities: read own\"   ON public.product_opportunities FOR SELECT USING (user_id = auth.uid());\nCREATE POLICY \"opportunities: insert own\" ON public.product_opportunities FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\nCREATE POLICY \"opportunities: update own\" ON public.product_opportunities FOR UPDATE USING (user_id = auth.uid()) WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\n\n-- opportunity_signals: per-user own-scoped + retention.\nALTER TABLE public.opportunity_signals ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nCREATE POLICY \"signals: read own\"   ON public.opportunity_signals FOR SELECT USING (user_id = auth.uid());\nCREATE POLICY \"signals: insert own\" ON public.opportunity_signals FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\n-- NOTA: signals NUNCA com user_id IS NULL (system nodes) \u2014 CHECK (user_id IS NOT NULL).\n```\n\n### 5.3 JWT Validation Points\n\nTodo edge fn user-facing segue o padr\u00e3o (SOP `docs/processes/edge-jwt-identity-verification.md`):\n```typescript\nconst authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization')\nif (!authHeader) return json(401, { error: 'unauthorized' })\nconst { data: { user }, error } = await supabase.auth.getUser(authHeader.replace('Bearer ', ''))\nif (error || !user) return json(401, { error: 'unauthorized' })\n// user.id \u00e9 o server-trusted owner \u2014 NUNCA confiar em user_id do body.\n```\n\n### 5.4 Secrets Inventory\n\n| Secret Name | Used In | Vault Location | Rotation Policy |\n|-------------|---------|----------------|----------------|\n| `amazon_tag` (n\u00e3o-secreto, mas per-user) | `resolve-affiliate-link` | `affiliate_config` (DB per-user) | User-controlled (BYOK) |\n| `amazon_creators_secret` *(Fatia 4)* | `amazon-creators-proxy` | Supabase Vault (per-user, cifrado) | User-controlled; token OAuth ~1h |\n| signal provider keys *(se algum exigir)* | `validate-opportunity` | per-user (`user_api_keys`-like) OU system infra key p/ cron (OTD-POE-007) | Per-user BYOK |\n\n> \u26a0\ufe0f **0 env global user-facing** para credencial de afiliado (API Tenancy Model). Nunca `Deno.env.get('AMAZON_*')` no fluxo user-facing.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Infrastructure Touchpoints\n\n| Infrastructure | Usage | Impact |\n|----------------|-------|--------|\n| `authenticated-browser-operator` (residential runner) | Login ef\u00eamero (Fatia 3); extrai Tracking ID/`amzn.to` | Novo perfil `isolated ephemeral` (OTD-POE-005); IP datacenter proibido p/ amazon/ML |\n| `orchestrate-content` (edge) | G5 conte\u00fado nativo por canal (self-bill) | Reuso; sem novo pipeline step |\n| Spaces `canvas-execute` / `avatarIdentity` | G4 avatar da persona | Reuso; char-ref sem-treino default Nano Banana |\n| `embed-mcorch-node` (edge) | Embed do n\u00f3 `observation` de oportunidade | N\u00f3 novo na malha; embed gr\u00e1tis |\n| `infra_health_logs` | Novos eventos `service='product-opportunity'` e `service='browser-operator'` | Watchdog telemetry (allowlist \u2014 sem PII/segredo) |\n| `mcorch_chroma` (ChromaDB) | N\u00e3o usado diretamente (embeddings via pgvector no `product_opportunities`) | Nenhum |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Migration Stubs\n\n```sql\n-- ============================================================\n-- MIGRATION 1: <ts>_poe_amazon_network_enum.sql  (Fatia 1)\n-- ============================================================\nALTER TYPE public.vm_affiliate_network ADD VALUE IF NOT EXISTS 'amazon';  -- padr\u00e3o de 20260628183500 (mercadopago)\n\n-- ============================================================\n-- MIGRATION 2: <ts>_poe_affiliate_config_amazon_tag.sql  (Fatia 1)\n-- ============================================================\nALTER TABLE public.affiliate_config ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS amazon_tag text;  -- BYOK per-user, fail-closed\n-- (RLS da affiliate_config j\u00e1 \u00e9 own-scoped; nenhuma policy nova. Safe-columns na UI omitem amazon_tag ap\u00f3s save.)\n\n-- ============================================================\n-- MIGRATION 3: <ts>_poe_affiliate_network_contract.sql  (Fatia 1) \u2014 reference/system data\n-- ============================================================\nCREATE TABLE public.affiliate_network_contract (\n  network                 public.vm_affiliate_network PRIMARY KEY,\n  tag_param               text NOT NULL,              -- 'matt_word' | 'tag'\n  cookie_window           text NOT NULL,              -- '24h+90d cart' | 'ml-opaque'\n  allows_redirect_cloaking boolean NOT NULL,          -- ML=true, Amazon=FALSE (\u00a76(v))\n  host_allowlist          text[] NOT NULL,            -- ['mercadolivre.com.br'] | ['amazon.com.br','amazon.com',...]\n  data_api SECURITY;\nCREATE POLICY \"network_contract: public read\" ON public.affiliate_network_contract FOR SELECT USING (true);\nINSERT INTO public.affiliate_network_contract VALUES\n  ('mercadolivre','matt_word','ml-opaque', true,  ARRAY['mercadolivre.com.br','meli.la'], NULL, now()),\n  ('amazon',      'tag',      '24h+90d cart', false, ARRAY['amazon.com.br','amazon.com','amzn.to'], 'creators-api', now());\n\n-- ============================================================\n-- MIGRATION 4: <ts>_poe_product_opportunities.sql  (Fatia 2)\n-- ============================================================\nCREATE TABLE public.product_opportunities (\n  id             uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,\n  user_id        uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL,\n  project_id     uuid REFERENCES public.projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n  niche          text,\n  product_url    text,\n  network        public.vm_affiliate_network,\n  stage          text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'G1',           -- G1..G6\n  verdict        text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'inconclusive', -- approved|rejected|inconclusive\n  verdict_text   text,\n  gates          jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,    -- per-gate pass + signal refs\n  node_id        uuid,                                  -- mcorch_nodes observation\n  embedding      vector(768),\n  stability_score float4 NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.5,\n  created_at     timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  updated_at     timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  CONSTRAINT niche_or_url CHECK (niche IS NOT NULL OR product_url IS NOT NULL)\n);\nALTER TABLE public.product_opportunities ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n-- (RLS policies \u00a75.2)\nCREATE INDEX product_opportunities_user_idx ON public.product_opportunities (user_id);\nCREATE INDEX product_opportunities_embedding_hnsw\n  ON public.product_opportunities USING hnsw (embedding vector_cosine_ops) WITH (m = 16, ef_construction = 64);\n\n-- ============================================================\n-- MIGRATION 5: <ts>_poe_opportunity_signals.sql  (Fatia 2) \u2014 ephemeral + provenance\n-- ============================================================\nCREATE TABLE public.opportunity_signals (\n  id                   uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,\n  opportunity_id       uuid REFERENCES public.product_opportunities(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL,\n  user_id              uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL,\n  gate                 text NOT NULL, text,                            -- provenance (AI Act/LGPD)\n  value                jsonb,                           -- extracted signal payload\n  degraded             boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,  -- fail-soft flag\n  retention_expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now() + interval '30 days',\n  created_at           timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  CONSTRAINT signals_not_system CHECK (user_id IS NOT NULL)   -- NUNCA system nodes\n);\nALTER TABLE public.opportunity_signals ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n-- (RLS policies \u00a75.2)\n```\n\n> **Todas as migrations acima passam `/security-review` (G-0.2) antes do commit.** A ADD VALUE do enum roda fora de transa\u00e7\u00e3o (padr\u00e3o da `20260628183500`).\n\n---\n\n## 8. Open Technical Decisions\n\n| ID | Description | Option A | Option B | Recommendation | Owner |\n|----|-------------|----------|----------|----------------|-------|\n| OTD-POE-001 | Contrato exato da Creators API (endpoint/shape/rate-limits n\u00e3o fetchados) | Fetchar doc oficial no ingest | Assumir shape de fontes secund\u00e1rias | **A** \u2014 n\u00e3o nomear a integra\u00e7\u00e3o antes de fetchar; dados Amazon = degrade at\u00e9 l\u00e1 | Sovereign |\n| OTD-POE-002 | Pol\u00edtica de redirect por rede | Flag `allowsRedirectCloaking` por rede | `/go` universal | **A (DECIDED)** \u2014 ML=true, Amazon=false | Sovereign |\n| OTD-POE-003 | Gate de 10 vendas/30d da Creators API | Conector funciona sem API (string-append) | Bloquear at\u00e9 gate | **A** \u2014 API s\u00f3 enriquece; link nunca depende dela | Sovereign |\n| OTD-POE-004 | Motor de Google Trends | API alpha oficial (aplicar acesso) | Captura human-paced | **A/B fail-soft** \u2014 nunca pytrends em prod; USD=0 gate | Sovereign |\n| OTD-POE-005 | Perfil ef\u00eamero no ABO | Emenda no ABO (novo perfil) | Vive no POE | **A** \u2014 perfil `isolated ephemeral` zero-persist como OTD-ABO | Sovereign |\n| OTD-POE-006 | Contabiliza\u00e7\u00e3o de venda vs clique por rede | Modelo de atribui\u00e7\u00e3o por rede | `affiliate_links.clicks` \u00fanico | **A** \u2014 ML=clique via `/go`; Amazon=venda (cookie), clique client-side/nativo | Sovereign |\n| OTD-POE-007 | Escopo dos sinais gratuitos (leitura vs automa\u00e7\u00e3o permitida) | Mapear fonte-a-fonte antes de automatizar | Automatizar tudo | **A** \u2014 per-fonte: manual/automa\u00e7\u00e3o-permitida/proibida | Sovereign |\n| OTD-POE-008 | `amzn.to` via ABO SiteStripe | Opt-in (conveni\u00eancia) | Caminho cr\u00edtico | **A** \u2014 `?tag=` cru j\u00e1 atribui venda; ABO opt-in | Sovereign |\n| OTD-POE-009 | Gate cl\u00e1ssico de standing Amazon (3 vendas/180d \u2014 n\u00e3o-confirmado) | Verificar + comunicar risco | Ignorar | **A** \u2014 comunicar risco de perda de standing | Sovereign |\n| OTD-POE-010 | Modelo de custo mcoCoins do funil | Por-gate | Bundle | Medir $ real por perna (SOP mcoin-cost-calibration) antes de fixar | Sovereign |\n\n---\n\n## 9. Fatiamento (implementa\u00e7\u00e3o \u2014 gated por \u00a70)\n\n| Fatia | Entrega | Gate material (Lei 1) |\n|-------|---------|------------------------|\n| **1** | Enum `amazon` + `affiliate_config.amazon_tag` + `affiliate_network_contract` + dispatcher + `resolve-affiliate-link` + UI colar-link + Settings Amazon | Colar link Amazon real \u2192 200 `?tag=` do user (`SELECT`); sem config \u2192 402 literal; `grep` 0 `/go` no path Amazon; `/security-review` SAFE |\n| **2** | Funil G1\u2013G3 (sinais gratuitos fail-soft) + `product_opportunities`/`opportunity_signals` + mesh mandate + proveni\u00eancia | Funil sobre 1 nicho \u2192 veredito com \u22652 fontes citadas + n\u00f3 UUID (`SELECT`); fail-soft provado (fonte fora do ar \u2192 `degraded`); `infra_health_logs` pulse |\n| **3** | Login ef\u00eamero zero-persist (ABO) + G4 avatar + G5 conte\u00fado + G6 workspace + consent LGPD | `ls` profile dir vazio p\u00f3s-`context.close()`; Tracking ID per-user (`SELECT`); criativo com `hook_rate`; E2E UI audit 1920\u00d71080 |\n| **4 (gated)** | Creators API (OAuth2 LwA per-user) + N-\u00e9sima rede | Chamada Creators API real (status+body) com gate 10 vendas/30d; degrade provado sem o gate. **GO Sovereign + OTD-POE-001/003 fechados** |\n\n---\n\n## 10. Testing / Observability\n\n- **Smokes re-execut\u00e1veis (zero-cost, herm\u00e9ticos)** \u2014 irm\u00e3os dos existentes (`smoke-asset-owner-signing`, etc.): `smoke-affiliate-dispatcher.ts` (roteamento por rede), `smoke-amazon-tag-append.ts` (**gate:** `?tag=` + 0 `/go` + host validado + own-tag-only 402), `smoke-opportunity-funnel.ts` (converg\u00eancia \u22652, fail-soft, tenant-bound), `smoke-ephemeral-login.ts` (0 arquivo em disco).\n- **Telemetria:** cada path (ok/degraded/error) emite em `infra_health_logs` (`service='product-opportunity'` / `'browser-operator'`) via chokepoint com allowlist (sem PII/segredo \u2014 padr\u00e3o `20260615170000`).\n- **Vision QA** (`scripts/qa/vision-qa.ts`, custo 0) sobre prints de inspe\u00e7\u00e3o de qualquer UI nova antes de declarar pronto (`feedback_vision_qa_always`).\n\n---\n\n## 11. Non-Functional Design Notes\n\n- **Fail-open vs fail-closed (cr\u00edtico):** monetiza\u00e7\u00e3o/link = **fail-closed** na credencial (402), **fail-open** no ledger (nunca quebra o pipeline por indisponibilidade de tracking) \u2014 espelha `monetizeForProduct`. Sinais = **fail-soft** (degraded). Estas tr\u00eas posturas s\u00e3o distintas e propositais.\n- **Anti-cloaking como invariante de tipo:** `cloaked` \u00e9 sempre `false` no retorno Amazon; o dispatcher n\u00e3o tem caminho que produza `/go` para `allows_redirect_cloaking=false`. Testado por `grep` + smoke.\n- **Reuso > inven\u00e7\u00e3o:** o POE quase n\u00e3o cria infra \u2014 orquestra `_shared/affiliate.ts`, `affiliate_config`, `vm_affiliate_products`, `orchestrate-content`, ABO, Spaces. Cada tabela nova \u00e9 justificada (verdade persistente do funil + proveni\u00eancia ef\u00eamera).\n\n---\n\n## 12. Pattern Conformance Declaration (21 padr\u00f5es \u00b7 `docs/architecture/agentic-vision.md` \u00a72)\n\n> **Gate Closed-Loop Step 3.5 (CLAUDE.md \u00a71).** Declara\u00e7\u00e3o, n\u00e3o sign-off. Contagem material **por-linha**; nenhum `yes` fabricado; nenhum `deferred` sem OTD/scope; nenhum `n-a` sem raz\u00e3o. Nomes copiados **verbatim** do doutrina. **Promovida verbatim** para [`09-pattern-conformance.md`](09-pattern-conformance.md) (vers\u00e3o autoritativa).\n\n| # | Pattern | Implemented? (yes/deferred/n-a) | How / Why-deferred |\n|---|---------|:------------------------------:|---|\n| 1 | Prompt Chaining | **yes** | Funil G1\u2192G6 = cadeia ordenada (cada gate destrava o pr\u00f3ximo \u2014 \u00a73 Use Case B); G5 reusa a cadeia `orchestrate-content`. Tra\u00e7a FR-POE-004/014 |\n| 2 | Routing | **yes** | Dispatcher `buildAffiliateUrl(network,\u2026)` roteia por rede lendo `affiliate_network_contract` (\u00a72.4 \u00b7 FR-POE-001/013). **Load-bearing** |\n| 3 | Parallelization | **deferred** | Coleta de sinais independentes por gate poderia fan-out (Trends\u2225Meta\u2225Reddit); bounded/fail-soft e sequencial no scope da Fatia 2 \u2014 sem benef\u00edcio agora (rastreado; runner residencial 1-tarefa/vez) |\n| 4 | Reflection | **yes** | Cross-check anti-ru\u00eddo (Movers&Shakers\u00d7Trends, estrutural vs c\u00edclico) + converg\u00eancia \u22652 = cr\u00edtico que recusa sinal isolado (\u00a7 regra de ouro \u00b7 FR-POE-004); Vision QA sobre criativos G5. **Load-bearing** |\n| 5 | Tool Use | **yes** | Google Trends/Meta Ad Library/Reddit/SERP/Creators API/agent-browser como tools; telemetria por path em `infra_health_logs` (\u00a76 \u00b7 FR-POE-016) |\n| 6 | Planning | **yes** | Os 6 gates ordenados progressivamente-mais-caros **s\u00e3o** o plano (hip\u00f3tese-primeiro por gate \u2014 Pilar IV); barato-primeiro mata cedo. Tra\u00e7a FR-POE-004 |\n| 7 | Multi-Agent | **deferred** | Reusa o coletivo a montante (Pantheon / `orchestrate-content` / Spaces avatar) em G4/G5; nenhum protocolo inter-agente novo \u00e9 autorado neste m\u00f3dulo (sem benef\u00edcio no scope) |\n| 8 | Memory Management | **yes** | N\u00f3 `observation` na Knowledge Mesh + verdade persistente `product_opportunities` (embedding 768d) + cache ef\u00eamero isolado `opportunity_signals`. Tra\u00e7a FR-POE-006. **Load-bearing** |\n| 9 | Learning & Adaptation | **deferred** | G5 emite `creative_metrics.hook_rate` = a costura de aprendizado; o loop RL fechado \u00e9 do ecossistema (OTD-AG-002), n\u00e3o deste m\u00f3dulo. Sem reward loop in-module |\n| 10 | Model Context Protocol | **deferred** | `resolve-affiliate-link`/`validate-opportunity` como superf\u00edcie MCP futura (reusaria Vision MCP server); sem benef\u00edcio no scope das Fatias 1\u20133 |\n| 11 | Goal Setting & Monitoring | **yes** | `product_opportunities.stage/verdict` por oportunidade + telemetria `service='product-opportunity'`; goal = \"qualificar antes de gastar\" (\u00a710 \u00b7 FR-POE-006/016) |\n| 12 | Exception Handling & Recovery | **yes** | Tr\u00eas posturas expl\u00edcitas: sinais **fail-soft** (degraded), credencial **fail-closed** (402), ledger **fail-open** (\u00a711); refund-on-failure nas pernas de IA. Tra\u00e7a FR-POE-003/016. **Load-bearing** |\n| 13 | Human-in-the-Loop | **yes** | Privado-primeiro (verdito+criativo nascem rascunho; Sovereign promove \u2014 BR-POE-007); usu\u00e1rio digita a pr\u00f3pria senha no login ef\u00eamero; GO gates \u00a70. Tra\u00e7a FR-POE-005. **Load-bearing** |\n| 14 | Knowledge Retrieval (RAG) | **n-a** | O m\u00f3dulo **escreve** na malha (n\u00f3 `observation` \u2014 padr\u00e3o #8) mas n\u00e3o recupera semanticamente dela em runtime como fun\u00e7\u00e3o central; consome sinais externos + cat\u00e1logo `vm_affiliate_products` (JOIN determin\u00edstico, n\u00e3o RAG) |\n| 15 | Inter-Agent Communication (A2A) | **n-a** | Sem intera\u00e7\u00e3o agente-agente; comunica\u00e7\u00e3o \u00e9 hook\u2194edge\u2194runner\u2194bin\u00e1rio e edge\u2194DB via RLS |\n| 16 | Resource-Aware Optimization | **yes** | mcoCoins 4\u00d7-floor per-perna + refund; sinais **USD=0** (mandato anti-Apify); BYOK anda de gra\u00e7a; runner residencial bounded. Tra\u00e7a BR-POE-004/012 \u00b7 OTD-POE-010. **Load-bearing** |\n| 17 | Reasoning Techniques | **deferred** | G3 s\u00edntese de persona a partir de review/Reddit mining usa LLM (motor reusado); nenhum framework formal de racioc\u00ednio (Graph-of-Thought) in-module no scope |\n| 18 | Guardrails / Safety | **yes** | O m\u00f3dulo **\u00c9** um guardrail de compliance: anti-cloaking por-rede (`allowsRedirectCloaking=false`), anti-scraping (valida\u00e7\u00e3o fora da Amazon), own-tag-only fail-closed, zero-persist do login, proveni\u00eancia/reten\u00e7\u00e3o dos sinais; + Survival Laws + Cyber-Sentinel + RLS default-deny (\u00a75). **Load-bearing** |\n| 19 | Evaluation & Monitoring | **yes** | Veredito exige **\u22652 sinais convergentes com URLs citadas** = avalia\u00e7\u00e3o material antes de reportar (FR-POE-004); Vision QA; coverage de fail-soft/zero-cloaking/zero-persist como m\u00e9tricas (`08-quality-metrics.md`) |\n| 20 | Prioritization | **deferred** | O fatiamento prioriza o gate mais barato/alto-recall primeiro (G1 antes das pernas de IA \u2014 \u00a79); scheduler formal entre oportunidades concorrentes n\u00e3o se aplica no scope (curadoria humana) |\n| 21 | Exploration & Discovery | **yes** | G1/G2 nicho\u2192produto = descoberta via Trends/Movers&Shakers/Meta Ad Library (linhagem `fetch-trends`/`vm_trends`, autopilot angle); explorar oportunidades **\u00e9** a fun\u00e7\u00e3o do \"c\u00e9rebro\" do m\u00f3dulo |\n\n**Placar declarado:** `yes` **13** (#1, #2, #4, #5, #6, #8, #11, #12, #13, #16, #18, #19, #21) \u00b7 `deferred` **6** (#3 Parallelization, #7 Multi-Agent, #9 Learning, #10 MCP, #17 Reasoning, #20 Prioritization) \u00b7 `n-a` **2** (#14 RAG, #15 A2A). **Soma material das 21 linhas = 13 + 6 + 2 = 21** (Lei 1 \u2014 contagem por-linha da tabela acima).\n\n**Padr\u00f5es load-bearing (a espinha da fun\u00e7\u00e3o):** **#2 Routing** (dispatcher multi-rede), **#18 Guardrails/Safety** (o m\u00f3dulo \u00c9 o guardrail de compliance ToS), **#4 Reflection** (converg\u00eancia \u22652 + cross-check anti-ru\u00eddo), **#12 Exception Handling** (fail-soft/fail-closed/fail-open), **#8 Memory Management** (verdade persistente + cache ef\u00eamero), **#13 HITL** (privado-primeiro + senha do pr\u00f3prio user), **#16 Resource-Aware** (USD=0 + mcoCoins), **#21 Exploration & Discovery** (o c\u00e9rebro descobre oportunidade).\n\n---\n\n_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0 \u2014 SDD design-level SSOT. **C\u00f3digo GATED (\u00a70):** BoK 9/9 + `/security-review` por migration + GO Sovereign + revis\u00e3o jur\u00eddica ToS Amazon + SOPs Lei 2. Supabase Physical Schema Stubs\n\n> Ver `05-sdd.md \u00a77` para os stubs SQL completos (5 migrations). Todas passam `/security-review` (G-0.2) antes do commit. Resumo:\n\n```sql\n-- 1. ALTER TYPE public.vm_affiliate_network ADD VALUE IF NOT EXISTS 'amazon';   -- (fora de txn)\n-- 2. ALTER TABLE public.affiliate_config ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS amazon_tag text;\n-- 3. CREATE TABLE public.opportunity_signals (...) + RLS own + CHECK(user_id IS NOT NULL) + retention default;\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. GraphRAG Knowledge Nodes\n\n### Node Types Introduced\n\n| node_type | Title Pattern | Content Fields | Embedding Source |\n|-----------|--------------|----------------|-----------------|\n| `observation` (reuse) | `opportunity: <niche/product>` | `verdict_text` + gates aprovados + fontes convergentes | `niche/product + verdict_text` (via `embed-mcorch-node`) |\n\n> **N\u00e3o** cria node_type novo \u2014 reusa `observation` (padr\u00e3o do mesh mandate). Uma oportunidade validada = uma observa\u00e7\u00e3o com proveni\u00eancia dos sinais.\n\n### Edge Types Introduced\n\n| Edge relationship | Source node_type | Target node_type | Metadata |\n|-------------------|-----------------|-----------------|----------|\n| `DERIVES_FROM` (reuse) | `observation` (opportunity) | `documentation_suite` (POE seal) | `{ fatia, network }` |\n| `ATTRIBUTES_REVENUE_TO` (candidate) | `observation` (opportunity) | product/affiliate node | per-network attribution (OTD-POE-006) |\n\n### Embedding Trigger\n\nAp\u00f3s INSERT bem-sucedido em `product_opportunities` (veredito aprovado), o edge fn chama:\n```typescript\nawait supabase.functions.invoke('embed-mcorch-node', {\n  body: { node_id: node.id, content: `${niche ?? product_url} ${verdict_text}` }\n})\n```\n> O trigger `trg_mcorch_embed_on_insert` (pg_net) tamb\u00e9m dispara para n\u00f3s inseridos direto. Embedding **gr\u00e1tis** (`MESH_EMBED_*` system key \u2014 n\u00e3o conta como custo user-facing).\n\n### FSRS-6 Parameters\n\n| Field | Initial Value | Decay Rule |\n|-------|--------------|------------|\n| `stability_score` | `0.5` | Decai por migration `20260425190000`; oportunidade re-validada \u2192 refor\u00e7o |\n| `revenue_impact` (no n\u00f3) | 0 at\u00e9 haver venda atribu\u00edda; > 0 quando comiss\u00e3o real registrada | Atualizado por atribui\u00e7\u00e3o per-rede (OTD-POE-006) |\n\n---\n\n## 5. Tenant Isolation Map\n\n| Table | `user_id` scope | `project_id` scope | System rows (user_id IS NULL) |\n|-------|----------------|-------------------|-------------------------------|\n| `affiliate_config` | \u2705 Required | \u2014 | No |\n| `affiliate_network_contract` | \u2014 (reference) | \u2014 | **Yes** (system reference data, public-read, service-role-write) |\n| `product_opportunities` | \u2705 Required | Optional | No |\n| `opportunity_signals` | \u2705 Required (**CHECK NOT NULL**) | \u2014 | **No \u2014 nunca** (proveni\u00eancia de terceiro jamais system) |\n| `vm_affiliate_products` | \u2014 (shared catalog) | \u2014 | Yes (cat\u00e1logo compartilhado) |\n| `mcorch_nodes` | Optional | Optional | Yes (system nodes) \u2014 mas oportunidade \u00e9 sempre per-user |\n\n> **Invariante FM-POE-09:** `opportunity_signals.user_id IS NOT NULL` \u00e9 um CHECK \u2014 sinais scrapeados de terceiros **nunca** viram dados system-shared nem entram na spine `creative_assets`.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Data Quality Rules\n\n| Table | Column | Rule | Violation Message (PT-BR) |\n|-------|--------|------|--------------------------|\n| `affiliate_config` | `amazon_tag` | formato Tracking ID Amazon (validar no ingest \u2014 OTD) | \"Tracking ID da Amazon inv\u00e1lido.\" |\n| `product_opportunities` | `niche`/`product_url` | pelo menos um NOT NULL (CHECK `niche_or_url`) | \"Descreva o nicho ou cole um link de produto.\" |\n| `product_opportunities` | `verdict` | \u2208 {approved, rejected, inconclusive} | (interno) |\n| `opportunity_signals` | `source` | \u2208 allowlist de fontes gratuitas ToS-safe (OTD-POE-007) | \"Fonte de sinal n\u00e3o permitida.\" |\n| `opportunity_signals` | `user_id` | CHECK NOT NULL (nunca system) | (interno \u2014 invariante FM-POE-09) |\n| `affiliate_network_contract` | `allows_redirect_cloaking` | Amazon SEMPRE `false` | (invariante FM-POE-03) |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Retention & Archival Policy\n\n| Data | Retention Period | Archival Strategy | Deletion Trigger |\n|------|-----------------|-------------------|-----------------|\n| `opportunity_signals` rows | **30 dias** (`retention_expires_at`) | Nenhum \u2014 sinais s\u00e3o ef\u00eameros por design (AI Act/LGPD) | Cron purge (`retention_expires_at < now()`) |\n| `product_opportunities` rows | **Indefinido** (verdade persistente, espelha `content_strategy`) | Embedding p/ recall | CASCADE do user; user-initiated delete (LGPD) |\n| `affiliate_config.amazon_tag` | User-controlled | \u2014 | `disconnect_amazon()` RPC (espelha `disconnect_wordpress`) |\n| Embeddings (`vector`) | Indefinido | Backfill `scripts/backfill-embeddings.ts` | Com a linha pai |\n| `infra_health_logs` | 90 dias | Nenhum | Cron purge |\n| **Login ef\u00eamero (sess\u00e3o)** | **0 (zero-persist)** | Nenhum \u2014 `storageState` em mem\u00f3ria | `context.close()` (imediato; FR-POE-005) |\n\n---\n\n_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0_\n"
