Process Inventory\n\n| Process ID | Name | Type | Trigger | Owner | SLA |\n|------------|------|------|---------|-------|-----|\n| PROC-POE-001 | Resolver link de afiliado (multi-rede) | automated | User cola link (UI) | User | < 400ms p95 |\n| PROC-POE-002 | Funil de valida\u00e7\u00e3o G1\u2013G3 | async / human-in-loop | User roda o funil | User | < 60s p95 at\u00e9 veredito |\n| PROC-POE-003 | Login ef\u00eamero de portal (ABO zero-persist) | human-in-loop | User aciona \"conectar via portal\" | User | Interativo (user autentica) |\n| PROC-POE-004 | Flywheel criativo G4\u2013G6 | automated + human-in-loop | Oportunidade aprovada | User | Por perna de IA |\n| PROC-POE-005 | Purga de reten\u00e7\u00e3o de sinais | cron | `retention_expires_at < now()` | System | Di\u00e1rio |\n\n> Types: `automated` | `human-in-loop` | `cron` | `event-driven`\n\n---\n\n## 2. BPMN-lite Process Maps\n\n### PROC-POE-001: Resolver link de afiliado (multi-rede)\n\n```\nSTART \u2500\u25b6 [Colar link ORIGINAL] \u2500\u25b6 <Gateway: detectNetwork(url)?>\n                                        \u2502 amazon                       \u2502 mercadolivre\n                                  [SELECT amazon_tag                [buildMlProductUrl\n                                   WHERE user=auth.uid()]            (meli.la \u2192 matt_word)]\n                                        \u2502                               \u2502\n                            <Gateway: tag presente?>                    \u2502\n                              \u2502 SIM            \u2502 N\u00c3O                     \u2502\n                     [buildAmazonProductUrl   [HTTP 402                 \u2502\n                      append ?tag=, strip     amazon_not_configured]    \u2502\n                      old tag, NO /go]         (fail-closed,            \u2502\n                              \u2502                own-tag-only)            \u2502\n                              \u2502                     \u2502                   \u2502\n                              \u25c0\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2534\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518\n                              \u2502\n                    [HTTP 200 {affiliate_url, cloaked:false}]\n                              \u2502\n                             END\n```\n\n**Swimlanes:**\n\n| Swimlane | Participant | Tasks |\n|----------|-------------|-------|\n| User | Browser / `PasteAffiliateLinkCard` | Cola link; copia o link resultante |\n| Frontend Hook | `useAffiliateResolve.ts` | POST JWT; exp\u00f5e rede + tag mascarada |\n| Edge Function | `resolve-affiliate-link` | detectNetwork; resolve cred per-user fail-closed; dispatcher |\n| SharedLib | `_shared/affiliate.ts` | `buildAffiliateUrl` \u2192 `buildMlProductUrl` / `buildAmazonProductUrl` |\n| DB | `affiliate_config`, `affiliate_network_contract` | SELECT per-user; contrato por rede |\n\n> **Sem External API** neste processo (Amazon = string-append; nenhum fetch contra amazon.*).\n\n### PROC-POE-002: Funil de valida\u00e7\u00e3o G1\u2013G3 (async, fail-soft)\n\n```\n[User Action: rodar funil sobre nicho]\n  \u2502\n  \u25bc\n[Hook: useOpportunityFunnel \u2192 POST validate-opportunity]\n  \u2502\n  \u25bc\n[Edge Fn: validate-opportunity]\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 Validate JWT \u2500\u2500(fail)\u2500\u2500\u25b6 HTTP 401\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 INSERT product_opportunities (queued) \u2500\u2500\u25b6 HTTP 202 {opportunity_id}\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 G1 Nicho: Google Trends (\u226512m) + Meta Ad Library\n  \u2502      \u2514\u2500(fonte fora do ar)\u2500\u25b6 marca degraded + telemetria (N\u00c3O crasha)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 G2 Produto: Movers&Shakers \u00d7 Trends cross-check (Creators API OR residencial \u2014 NUNCA datacenter\u2192amazon)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 G3 Persona: review/Reddit mining \u2192 dores\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 <Gateway: converg\u00eancia \u22652 sinais independentes?>\n  \u2502      \u2502 SIM \u2500\u25b6 verdict='approved' + fontes citadas\n  \u2502      \u2502 N\u00c3O \u2500\u25b6 verdict='rejected'|'inconclusive' (kill/pivot barato, 0 mco)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 INSERT opportunity_signals (+ proveni\u00eancia + retention 30d)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 Embed observation node via embed-mcorch-node\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500 UPDATE product_opportunities (terminal)\n  \u2502\n  \u25bc\n[Poll: validate-opportunity-poll] \u2500\u25b6 veredito terminal + URLs\n```\n\n### PROC-POE-003: Login ef\u00eamero de portal (ABO zero-persist)\n\n```\n[User Action: \"conectar Amazon via portal\"]\n  \u2502\n  \u25bc\n[Consent gate: affiliate_operator_consents (LGPD)] \u2500\u2500(sem consent)\u2500\u2500\u25b6 bloqueia\n  \u2502\n  \u25bc\n[Residential runner (agent-browser) \u2014 isolated ephemeral context, storageState EM MEM\u00d3RIA]\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 abre a p\u00e1gina de login do portal\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 USU\u00c1RIO digita a pr\u00f3pria senha (agente NUNCA a recebe/transmite)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 extrai s\u00f3 o artefato N\u00c3O-secreto (Tracking ID / amzn.to)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 context.close() \u2500\u2500\u25b6 descarta cookie/sess\u00e3o/senha (0 bytes em disco)\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500 grava artefato per-user (JWT MCORCH) em affiliate_config.amazon_tag / metadata.shortlinks\n  \u2502\n  \u25bc\nEND  (prova: `ls` do profile dir vazio)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. n8n / Runner Workflow Blueprints\n\n### Runner: Residential Ephemeral Login (PROC-POE-003)\n\n**Trigger:** user action (n\u00e3o cron \u2014 exige intera\u00e7\u00e3o humana)\n**Idempotency key:** `user_id + portal + timestamp` (uma sess\u00e3o descart\u00e1vel por invoca\u00e7\u00e3o)\n\n```\n[Trigger: user \"conectar portal\"]\n  \u2502\n  \u25bc\n[Node: launch isolated ephemeral context (storageState in-memory, path:NULL)]\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 (residential IP only \u2014 datacenter \u2192 amazon/ML = 403)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 user authenticates in own browser\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 (success) \u2500\u2500\u25b6 [extract non-secret artifact] \u2500\u2500\u25b6 [context.close()] \u2500\u2500\u25b6 [store per-user via JWT]\n  \u2502                                                          \u2502\n  \u2502                                                    [verify: ls profile dir empty]\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500 (error/timeout) \u2500\u2500\u25b6 [context.close()] \u2500\u2500\u25b6 [infra_health_logs {service:'browser-operator', event:'ephemeral_login_failed'}]\n                                                     \u2502\n                                               [no partial credential persisted]\n```\n\n**Data Mapping:**\n\n| Input Field | Source | \u2192 | Output Field | Destination |\n|-------------|--------|---|--------------|-------------|\n| user JWT | MCORCH session | \u2192 | owner identity | `affiliate_config.user_id` |\n| Tracking ID (extra\u00eddo) | portal DOM | \u2192 | `amazon_tag` | `affiliate_config` |\n| `amzn.to` (opt-in) | SiteStripe | \u2192 | `metadata.shortlinks[external_id]` | `affiliate_config` |\n| senha do user | **NUNCA capturada** | \u2192 | \u2717 | \u2717 (jamais ao servidor) |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Orchestration Integration Points\n\n> O POE **n\u00e3o** injeta um step novo no `orchestrate-content` \u2014 ele **invoca** o pipeline existente em G5 (reuso, n\u00e3o extens\u00e3o). A monetiza\u00e7\u00e3o j\u00e1 acontece dentro do pipeline via `_shared/affiliate.ts` (agora multi-rede).\n\n| Pipeline Step | Step ID | Position | Description | mcoCoins Trigger |\n|---------------|---------|----------|-------------|-----------------|\n| G5 Conte\u00fado (reuso) | (orchestrate-content existente) | Invocado p\u00f3s-G4 | Gera criativo nativo por canal; monetiza com o link per-user (dispatcher multi-rede) | Self-bill do pipeline (10 mco) |\n\n**Integration note (dentro de `_shared/affiliate.ts`, j\u00e1 vivo, agora multi-rede):**\n```typescript\n// monetizeForProduct(...) resolve o link via buildAffiliateUrl(network,\u2026):\n//   network='mercadolivre' \u2192 meli.la/matt_word (existente, /go OK)\n//   network='amazon'       \u2192 product_url + ?tag= (NOVO, CRU, sem /go)\n// A escolha de rede vem do produto (vm_affiliate_products.network), n\u00e3o de if espalhado.\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Error Handling & Retry Strategy\n\n| Process | Step | Error Type | Max Retries | Backoff | Failure Action |\n|---------|------|-----------|-------------|---------|----------------|\n| PROC-POE-001 | resolve cred | Amazon n\u00e3o configurado | 0 | \u2014 | **HTTP 402 fail-closed** (nunca tag global) |\n| PROC-POE-001 | detectNetwork | host n\u00e3o reconhecido | 0 | \u2014 | HTTP 422 \"Cole um link v\u00e1lido\" |\n| PROC-POE-002 | sinal G1/G2/G3 | fonte fora do ar / 429 / CAPTCHA | 1 (human-paced) | Fixo | **fail-soft `degraded`** + telemetria; exige 2\u00ba sinal (nunca crash, nunca falso-verde) |\n| PROC-POE-002 | perna de IA (G4+) | falha de gera\u00e7\u00e3o | idempotente | \u2014 | **refund-on-failure** (`add_mco_coins`); `infra_health_logs` |\n| PROC-POE-003 | login ef\u00eamero | timeout/erro | 0 | \u2014 | `context.close()` + 0 credencial parcial persistida |\n| Any | JWT | token inv\u00e1lido | 0 | \u2014 | HTTP 401 imediato |\n\n> **Regra de ouro (FM-POE-07):** nenhum sinal isolado aprova. Converg\u00eancia \u22652 \u00e9 o gate; falha em convergir = `inconclusive`, n\u00e3o `approved`.\n\n---\n\n## 6. SLA Definitions\n\n| Process | Step | Expected Duration | Timeout Threshold | Alert if exceeded |\n|---------|------|------------------|-------------------|-------------------|\n| PROC-POE-001 | End-to-end (string-append) | < 400ms | 3s | Yes \u2192 infra_health_logs |\n| PROC-POE-002 | End-to-end (async job) | < 60s | 180s | Yes (job \u00f3rf\u00e3o \u2192 reconcile no poll) |\n| PROC-POE-002 | 1 sinal gratuito | < 15s | 30s | No (fail-soft degraded) |\n| PROC-POE-003 | Login interativo | user-paced | 10min (state TTL) | Yes \u2192 limpa contexto |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Cron Jobs\n\n| Job Name | Schedule (cron) | Function | Idempotency | Failure Alerting |\n|----------|----------------|----------|-------------|-----------------|\n| `poe-signal-retention-purge` | `0 4 * * *` (di\u00e1rio) | DELETE `opportunity_signals` WHERE `retention_expires_at < now()` | `retention_expires_at` (re-run = mesmo resultado) | `infra_health_logs service='product-opportunity'` |\n| `poe-orphan-job-reconcile` | (on-poll, self-heal) | Reconcilia `product_opportunities` \u00f3rf\u00e3s (queued > SLA) \u2192 `inconclusive` + refund se aplic\u00e1vel | por `opportunity_id` | `infra_health_logs` |\n\n> Todos idempotentes. A purga de reten\u00e7\u00e3o \u00e9 o mecanismo material do AI Act/LGPD para sinais ef\u00eameros (nunca acumulam).\n\n---\n\n_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0_\n"
}

SLA / SLO / SLI Definitions\n\n| Service | SLI | SLO | SLA |\n|---------|-----|-----|-----|\n| `resolve-affiliate-link` | p95 latency | \u2264 400ms | Investigar se > 3s |\n| `validate-opportunity` | % jobs com veredito dentro do SLA | \u2265 95% | Reconcile \u00f3rf\u00e3os no poll |\n| Signal freshness | % sinais purgados dentro de 30d+1 | 100% | Cron purge di\u00e1rio |\n| Compliance | ban/suspens\u00e3o de afiliado | **0** | Halt + revis\u00e3o jur\u00eddica imediata |\n| Zero-persist login | profile dir vazio p\u00f3s-close | 100% | Witness por invoca\u00e7\u00e3o |\n\n---\n\n## 7. > === Lei 1 consistency: every 'PA-API 5.0' mention must be banned/dead context ===
> 01-mrd.md:12:Afiliados e criadores de conteúdo enfrentam **dois custos escondidos** antes de qualquer real de comissão: (a) o **risco de investir tempo/dinheiro num produto morto** (sem demanda estrutural real), e (b) a **fricção de gerar o próprio link de afiliado compliant** em cada rede — cada rede com formato de tag, janela de cookie e política de cloaking diferentes. O **Product Opportunity Engine (POE)** funde as duas metades num flywheel: um **funil de validação orgânica gratuita** (nicho → produto → persona → avatar → conteúdo → workspace, gates ordenados progressivamente mais caros) que mata oportunidades ruins **antes** do gasto, acoplado a um **rail multi-afiliado per-user** que devolve ao usuário **o link de afiliado DELE** a partir de um link de produto original — começando pela Amazon Associates (Sovereign já aprovado), via a mecânica mais simples e mais compliant que a pesquisa estabeleceu: **string-append `?tag=` sale-attributable**. O momento é agora porque a premissa técnica original mudou: a **PA-API 5.0 morreu** (→ Creators API), e o achado material é que **a geração de link nem precisa de API** — é anexação de parâmetro server-side, custo USD = 0.
> 01-mrd.md:69:1. **Mudança regulatória de API (Amazon):** a PA-API 5.0 foi **desligada** (deprecação 15/05/2026, prazo estendido 30/06/2026 — já no passado em 2026-07-23). Qualquer integração escrita sobre ela nasce morta. O achado material — **o link básico Amazon dispensa API** (string-append `?tag=` sale-attributable) — abre uma via mais simples E mais barata que o rail ML já provado. Fonte: [Amazon PA-API docs](https://webservices.amazon.com/paapi5/documentation/), [Amazon Program Policies](https://affiliate-program.amazon.com/help/operating/policies).
> 02-brd.md:107:- **PA-API 5.0 é proibida** no design (morta). Amazon = string-append `?tag=` ou Creators API (Fatia 4).

## Blueprint verdicts honored (verified by grep)

1. **PA-API 5.0 death** — SDD (`05-sdd.md`) has **zero** "PA-API 5.0" mentions; it names only the **Creators API (OAuth 2.0 Login-with-Amazon)**. Every other mention (MRD/BRD/index/FMEA) is explicitly framed as *dead/banned*, never a design choice.
2. **Amazon `?tag=` append = Fatia 1** — modeled as `buildAmazonProductUrl` (string-append, server-side, no API, no browser), `resolve-affiliate-link` at 0 mcoCoins.
3. **Anti-cloaking per-network flag** — `allows_redirect_cloaking` (ML=true, Amazon=false) present in SDD, FRD, and data-model; seeded in `affiliate_network_contract`; `cloaked:false` as a return invariant; guard-hit telemetry is P0.
4. **Free-organic validation lives outside Amazon** — G1–G3 use Trends/Meta Ad Library/Reddit/SERP; Amazon-specific signals only via Creators API or residential human-paced; `opportunity_signals.user_id IS NOT NULL` CHECK keeps scraped data out of system nodes/spine.

## Key design decisions carried through
- **Reuse over invention:** extends `_shared/affiliate.ts` (dispatcher + `NETWORK_CONTRACTS`), `affiliate_config` (+`amazon_tag`), `vm_affiliate_products` (enum +`amazon`), `orchestrate-content` (G5), `authenticated-browser-operator` (ephemeral zero-persist profile), Spaces (G4/G6). New tables limited to `affiliate_network_contract`, `product_opportunities`, `opportunity_signals`.
- **Per-user fail-closed** (API Tenancy Model): Amazon tag resolved by `auth.uid()`, 402 `amazon_not_configured`, zero global env in user-facing paths.
- **Code is explicitly GATED** (SDD §0): BoK 9/9 + `/security-review` per migration + Sovereign GO + **legal review of Amazon ToS** + 3 Lei-2 SOPs — stated as an unbreakable table with material proof required per gate.

IDs carried over verbatim: `FR-POE-001..016`, `OTD-POE-001..010`, `FM-POE-01..12` (with the blueprint's exact S×O×D/RPN values). Cross-references are consistent across all 9 docs (MR→BR→PR→FR→DB/EdgeFn chains resolve; zero orphans). Phase 7 (Knowledge Mesh seal via `scripts/seed-product-opportunity-engine-bok-seal.ts`) remains pending under Sovereign GO, as required.
